Without task-specific model training, our official submissions achieved third place in Visual MCQ with 0.7108 accuracy and first place in Visual OpenQA with 0.6488 COMET, 0.1391 BLEU, 0.2762 ROUGE L, and 0.2383 METEOR. The results highlight the practical value of inference engineering: careful scoring, ensembling, prompting, and cleanup can turn strong VLMs into reliable competition systems.

</details>


### [171] [Generative Brownian Bridge Diffusion In Motion Space For Enhanced Myocardial Strain Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01677)
*Rishov Paul,Frederick H. Epstein,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于布朗桥扩散模型的方法，从常规采集的心脏磁共振（CMR）图像中合成高质量的运动衍生应变值，无需专门的应变成像采集，显著提高了标准CMR的运动预测和应变分析精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的心肌应变分析技术要么需要人工后处理且区域精度不佳，要么需要专门且昂贵的成像采集。作者希望利用生成模型从常规CMR序列中合成高质量的应变值，以实现低成本、可临床部署的心脏功能评估工具。

Method: 提出一种在运动空间中训练的布朗桥扩散模型，学习从标准CMR注册方法估计的运动到先进应变成像技术提供的高精度运动之间的概率映射。模型以对应的CMR图像为条件，以促进生成过程中解剖结构的保真度。

Result: 在大规模多中心CMR数据集（包含标准电影CMR和先进应变成像配对采集）上验证，实验结果表明相比现有基于学习的方法，该框架显著提高了标准CMR的运动预测和应变分析精度。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 该研究代表了开发经济高效、可临床部署的AI心脏功能评估工具的新范式，能够在繁忙的临床工作流程中以增强的应变精度进行评估。

Abstract: Myocardial strain analysis of cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) images provides an important tool for evaluating cardiac function. However, current techniques require either human-adjusted post-processing with suboptimal regional accuracy, or specialized and costly imaging acquisitions. In this paper, we propose to leverage the power of generative models to synthesize high-quality motion-derived strain values from routinely acquired CMR sequences. Specifically, we develop a novel Brownian bridge diffusion model in motion space to learn the probabilistic mapping between standard CMR motion estimated from widely adopted registration methods and highly accurate motion provided by advanced strain imaging techniques. To promote the fidelity of anatomical structure in the generation process, our model is conditioned on the corresponding CMR images. We validate our method on large-scale multi-center CMR datasets including subjects of paired standard cine CMR and advanced strain imaging acquisitions. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly improves the accuracy of motion prediction and strain analysis from standard CMRs compared to existing learning-based approaches. Our research represents a new paradigm for potentially developing cost-effective, clinically deployable AI tools for cardiac function assessment with enhanced strain accuracy in busy clinical workflows. Our code is publicly available at anonymous.4open.science/r/Brownian-Bridge-strain-analysis-1140.

</details>


### [172] [Generative AI and Foundation Models in Medical Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01686)
*Masahiro Oda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文概述了生成式AI（扩散模型和大型语言模型）在医学支持领域的应用，并探讨了基础模型的构建方法及其在医疗领域的应用，最后讨论了如何利用国家数据和计算资源开发面向医疗支持的高性能基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和基础模型正在深刻改变医学图像处理及医疗AI开发的范式，需要对这一快速发展的领域进行系统梳理，并为利用国家资源和数据开发高性能医疗AI提供指导。

Method: 本文通过文献综述的方式，分别概述了图像生成AI中使用的扩散模型和文本生成AI中使用的大型语言模型（LLM），介绍它们在医疗支持中的具体应用，讨论基础模型的构建方法，并提出利用国家数据和计算资源开发医疗基础模型的思路。

Result: 文章系统介绍了扩散模型和LLM在医疗支持（如诊断报告生成和总结）中的应用，探讨了基础模型在医疗领域的构建与应用方法，并提出了基于国家数据和计算资源开发医疗基础模型的路径。

Conclusion: 生成式AI和基础模型为医学支持带来了新的发展范式，通过充分利用国家层面的数据和计算资源，可以构建高性能的医疗领域基础模型，推动AI在医疗中的深度应用。

Abstract: In recent years, generative AI has attracted significant public attention, and its use has been rapidly expanding across a wide range of domains. From creative tasks such as text summarization, idea generation, and source code generation, to the streamlining of medical support tasks like diagnostic report generation and summarization, AI is now deeply involved in many areas. Today's breadth of AI applications is clearly distinct from what was seen before generative AI gained widespread recognition. Representative generative AI services include DALL-E 3 (OpenAI, California, USA) and Stable Diffusion (Stability AI, London, England, UK) for image generation, ChatGPT (OpenAI, California, USA), and Gemini (Google, California, USA) for text generation. The rise of generative AI has been influenced by advances in deep learning models and the scaling up of data, models, and computational resources based on the scaling laws. Moreover, the emergence of foundation models, which are trained on large-scale datasets and possess general-purpose knowledge applicable to various downstream tasks, is creating a new paradigm in AI development. These shifts brought about by generative AI and foundation models also profoundly impact medical image processing, fundamentally changing the framework for AI development in healthcare. This paper provides an overview of diffusion models used in image generation AI and large language models (LLMs) used in text generation AI, and introduces their applications in medical support. This paper also discusses foundation models, which are gaining attention alongside generative AI, including their construction methods and applications in the medical field. Finally, the paper explores how to develop foundation models and high-performance AI for medical support by fully utilizing national data and computational resources.

</details>


### [173] [Entity-Aware Sequence Transduction for Player-Centric Ball Action Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01696)
*Ruifeng Wang,Di Yang,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多实体去噪序列转导（ME-DST）模型，通过在整个编码过程中保留角色槽位维度，使用时间注意力建模每个角色的历史演化、空间注意力跨角色交换信息，解决现有去噪序列转导基线在球员中心足球事件检测中因展平帧级表示而丢失球员特定时序演化和交互归纳偏置的问题。在FOOTPASS数据集上达到0.778的Micro F1，比最强官方基线提升10.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DST基线将球员角色维度展平为帧级表示，导致模型缺乏建模球员个体时序演化和球员间交互的归纳偏置，无法很好处理拥挤、部分遮挡的多智能体体育视频中的球员中心球类事件检测。

Method: ME-DST保持角色槽位维度贯穿编码过程：通过时间注意力建模每个角色槽位的历史演化，通过空间注意力在每帧内跨角色槽位交换信息，形成分离的因子化设计。同时引入可学习的角色嵌入、追踪派生的战术特征，以及融合X3D-L和Swin3D-S的视觉预测。

Result: 在FOOTPASS数据集上，ME-DST达到Micro F1为0.778，比最强官方TAAD+DST基线提升10.3个百分点。受控消融实验表明，保留实体轴和编码角色身份是性能提升的关键。

Conclusion: 显式的实体建模是球员中心体育事件理解的有效归纳偏置，通过将时序演化与交互上下文分离建模，可以显著提升事件检测与球员归属的性能。

Abstract: Player-centric ball action spotting requires temporally precise event detection together with actor attribution in crowded, partially observed multi-agent sports videos. Existing Denoising Sequence Transduction (DST) baselines treat the player-role dimension as part of a flattened frame-level representation, which weakens the inductive bias for modeling player-specific temporal evolution and inter-player interactions. To address this limitation, we propose Multi-Entity Denoising Sequence Transduction (ME-DST). ME-DST keeps the role-slot dimension throughout encoding. It uses temporal attention to model the history of each role slot, and spatial attention to exchange information across role slots at each frame. This factorized design gives the model a direct structure for separating within-player evolution from inter-player context. We also add learnable role embeddings, tracking-derived tactical features, and fused visual predictions from X3D-L and Swin3D-S. Experiments on the FOOTPASS dataset show that ME-DST reaches a Micro F1 of 0.778. This improves the strongest official TAAD+DST baseline by 10.3 percentage points. Controlled ablations show that preserving the entity axis and encoding role identity are central to this gain. These results suggest that explicit entity modeling is an effective inductive bias for player-centric sports event understanding.

</details>


### [174] [UniSim-SLAM: Feed-Forward SLAM with Unified Sim(3) Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01706)
*Inha Lee,Dongjae Jeong,Junhee Lee,Kyungdon Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniSim-SLAM是一个集成系统，前端用轻量级两视角关键帧跟踪，后端进行周期性多视角子图细化，通过统一的Sim(3)多层级因子图联合优化全局关键帧位姿和子图位姿，解决了几何基础模型在多视图中预测不一致和长序列轨迹漂移的问题，在TUM RGB-D和7-Scenes数据集上分别将轨迹误差降低了38.5%和45.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何基础模型虽然能实现SLAM的前馈推断，但其预测强烈依赖输入视角集合，导致几何不一致和长序列拼接时的轨迹漂移。同时在线部署时，两视角跟踪的低延迟与多视角推断的约束丰富性之间存在权衡矛盾。

Method: 提出UniSim-SLAM系统：前端运行轻量级两视角关键帧跟踪，后端周期性进行多视角子图细化。为了融合异质局部坐标系和不同尺度下的预测，构建统一的Sim(3)多层级因子图，联合优化全局关键帧位姿与子图位姿，图中整合了时间视角间里程计边、视角-子图桥接边（带深度统计尺度锚定）以及子图间连边和尺度约束。

Result: 在TUM RGB-D和7-Scenes数据集上，UniSim-SLAM在未标定设置下达到最先进精度，相比此前最优结果，TUM RGB-D轨迹误差降低38.5%，7-Scenes降低45.9%。

Conclusion: 通过前端两视角跟踪与后端多视角子图细化的集成以及Sim(3)统一因子图优化，有效解决了几何基础模型在SLAM中的视角依赖问题和尺度不一致问题，显著提升了长序列中的定位精度。

Abstract: Recent geometric foundation models enable feed-forward inference for SLAM, but their predictions are strongly dependent on the input view set, which leads to geometric inconsistencies and trajectory drift when results are chained over long sequences. Online deployment further exposes a trade-off between the low latency of two-view tracking and the constraint richness of multi-view inference. We introduce UniSim-SLAM, an integrated system that runs lightweight two-view keyframe tracking in the frontend and performs periodic multi-view submap refinement in the backend. To combine predictions defined in heterogeneous local coordinates with inconsistent scales, we formulate a unified multi-level factor graph on $Sim(3)$ that jointly optimizes global keyframe poses and submap poses. The graph integrates temporal view-to-view odometry edges, view-to-submap bridge edges with depth-statistics scale anchoring, and submap-to-submap tie and scale constraints to enforce consistent similarity relations across submaps. Experiments on TUM RGB-D and 7-Scenes show that UniSim-SLAM achieves state-of-the-art accuracy in the uncalibrated setting, reducing trajectory error by $38.5\% $ on TUM RGB-D and $45.9\%$ on 7-Scenes compared to prior best results. Project page: https://vision3d-lab.github.io/unisim-slam/

</details>


### [175] [SpatialQuery: Benchmarking Geometry-Grounded Multi-Instance Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01709)
*Hai Nguyen,Tung Vu,Cong Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPATIALQUERY，一个无需训练的框架，用于解决视觉语言模型在度量空间推理中的不足，特别是同类别多实例的距离比较问题。框架通过场景立方体化恢复实例级度量几何，并结合不确定性感知链式思维提示，在SPATIALQUERY-1M基准上超越了微调的空间专用模型和闭源前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在语义理解上表现优异，但在度量空间推理上不可靠，尤其是在同一物体类别中比较多个实例距离时。现有方法缺乏对实例级度量几何的恢复和不确定性处理能力。

Method: 提出SPATIALQUERY训练-free框架：包括Scene Cubifying（场景立方体化）将物体表示为统一尺寸、类别编码的方块以强调平面位置关系；以及不确定性感知链式思维（UA-CoT）提示，将几何推导的逐实例不确定性融入VLM推理过程。同时构建SPATIALQUERY-1M基准（200个室内场景，超过100万个RGB问答对）。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B上实现Floor-MAE 0.259米，Unc-Acc@0.3m 90.5%，邻近决策准确率84.18%，优于微调的空间专用模型、通用VLM和闭源前沿模型。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了无需特定任务微调或架构修改，仅通过几何先验和不确定性提示即可显著提升VLM的度量空间推理能力，为空间智能提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong semantic understanding but remain unreliable in metric spatial reasoning, particularly when queries require comparing multiple instances of the same object category. We study this problem through the Closest-Instance Distance Query (CIDQ), where a model must identify the nearest visible candidate to a unique reference object and estimate their gravity-aligned floor-plane distance. We introduce SPATIALQUERY, a training- free framework for CIDQ reasoning from a single RGB image, together with SPATIALQUERY-1M, a benchmark containing over one million RGB-only question-answer pairs from 200 indoor scenes. SPATIALQUERY recovers instance-level metric geometry and transforms it into a canonical Bird's-Eye View through Scene Cubifying, which represents objects as uniformly sized, category-coded blocks to emphasize their relative floor- plane locations. We further propose Uncertainty-Aware Chain-of-Thought (UA-CoT) prompting, which incorporates geometry- derived per-instance uncertainty into the VLM reasoning process. Without task-specific fine-tuning or architectural modification, SPATIALQUERY with Qwen3-VL-8B achieves a Floor-MAE of 0.259 m, an Unc-Acc@0.3 m of 90.5%, and a proximity-decision accuracy of 84.18%, outperforming fine-tuned spatial specialists, general-purpose VLMs, and closed-source frontier models. Code, benchmark resources, and an interactive demo are available at https://namhai1810.github.io/SpatialQuery/.

</details>


### [176] [STC-Net: Electroluminescence-Based Solar Cell Crack Segmentation for Power Loss Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01714)
*Shanaka Ramesh Gunasekara,Akila Eranda Devanarayana,Imasha Guruge,Nuwantha Fernando,Ehsan Asadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STC-Net网络，融合边缘先验、光谱先验和边界-拓扑细化模块，用于光伏EL图像中裂纹缺陷的分割，并将分割结果扩展到功率损耗估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏EL图像中的裂纹缺陷具有细长、结构受限的特点，现有分割方法难以准确捕捉此类缺陷的连续性和边界完整性。

Method: STC-Net包含三个核心组件：边缘先验、光谱先验和边界-拓扑细化模块；此外，通过从预测掩码中推导裂纹相关的非活性区域代理，将分割扩展到功率损耗估计。

Result: 在PVEL-S数据集上，训练阶段达到95.98 MIoU、98.01 MDice和98.00 MAcc；在未见测试样本上达到72.52 MIoU和80.16 MDice。

Conclusion: STC-Net实现了准确的裂纹定位，并为EL缺陷分割与光伏退化评估之间提供了实用的联系。

Abstract: Accurate crack assessment in electroluminescence (EL) images is important for photovoltaic (PV) reliability analysis, yet existing segmentation methods often fail to capture the thin, elongated, and structurally constrained nature of crack defects. This paper proposes a Solar Topology Crack Network (STC-Net) that incorporates edge priors, spectral priors, and a boundary-topology refinement module to improve crack continuity and boundary preservation. The framework further extends segmentation to power-loss estimation by deriving a crack-associated inactive-area proxy from the predicted masks. Experiments on the PVEL-S dataset show that STC-Net achieves 95.98 MIoU, 98.01 MDice, and 98.00 MAcc during training, and 72.52 MIoU and 80.16 MDice on unseen test samples. These results demonstrate that STC-Net provides accurate crack localization while offering a practical link between EL-based defect segmentation and PV degradation assessment.

</details>


### [177] [When Extreme Darkness Meets Motion Blur: MeanFlow for Unified RAW Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01720)
*Zepu Wang,Jingze Liang,Weijie Xiao,Kexin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向真实运动退化场景的极低光RAW图像增强框架，包含新数据集SIDED、统一RAW tokenizer和MeanFlow增强方法，并引入物理引导的精炼模型，实现了在耦合运动和噪声退化下的最优增强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极低光RAW增强方法主要关注光照和噪声问题，而忽略了实际低光成像中普遍存在的运动退化（如相机抖动、物体运动等），导致在真实场景下性能受限。

Method: 1) 构建SIDED数据集，对极低光RAW数据施加受控运动退化并保留原始传感器噪声；2) 提出统一的RAW tokenizer，配备显式域条件表征校准以对齐极低光和正常曝光RAW数据；3) 引入MeanFlow在一次函数求值中完成增强；4) 设计物理引导的精炼模型，增强光照-反射一致性、像素保真度和色彩保持，且不增加推理成本。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架在极低光RAW增强任务上达到了最先进的性能，并能够鲁棒地处理运动与噪声耦合退化的场景。

Conclusion: 这是首个在真实运动退化采集条件下建模极低光RAW增强问题并使用MeanFlow解决的工作，为实用低光成像提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Extremely low-light RAW enhancement aims to recover severely attenuated sensor signals, yet existing methods often focus on illumination and noise while overlooking the motion-induced degradations inherent in practical low-light imaging. We present a framework for robust extremely low-light RAW enhancement under realistic acquisition degradations. First, we introduce See in the Degraded Extremely Dark (SIDED), a new dataset that applies controlled motion degradation to extremely low-light RAW pairs while retaining their original sensor noise. Second, we propose a unified RAW tokenizer equipped with explicit domain-conditioned representation calibration to align extremely low-light and well-exposed RAW data, followed by a MeanFlow that performs enhancement in a single function evaluation. To our knowledge, this is the first work to formulate extremely low-light RAW enhancement under realistic motion-degraded acquisition and address it with MeanFlow. We further introduce a physics-guided refinement model to strengthen illumination--reflectance consistency, pixel fidelity, and color preservation without incurring additional inference cost. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance in extremely low-light RAW enhancement, and robustly handles coupled motion and noise degradations.

</details>


### [178] [G-Skin: Learning to Bind 3D Gaussians with Generative Visual Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01726)
*Yuxin Yao,Kendong Liu,Shiqi Zhou,Jiazhi Xia,Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G-Skin提出了一种新颖的生成式皮肤绑定框架，用于3D高斯表示的高保真动画。该方法通过引入骨架可控的图像生成模型来蒸馏2D视觉基础模型的运动先验，克服3D骨骼绑定数据稀缺的问题，并通过优化管线结合几何感知正则化实现平滑、结构一致的皮肤权重学习，在多个实验中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯溅射在真实感渲染中取得了巨大成功，但直接为任意骨架拓扑绑定这些3D高斯资产非常困难。原因在于：缺乏高质量的3D高斯绑定数据集使得前馈皮肤绑定框架训练不可行；而将基于网格的技术迁移到3D高斯表示时，由于高斯基元不受表面限制且缺乏显式拓扑连接导致泛化能力差；获取高质量绑定数据的成本又极其高昂。

Method: G-Skin框架包括两个核心部分：(1)引入一个骨架可控的图像生成模型，利用2D视觉基础模型将强大的运动先验蒸馏为伪指导，解决3D数据稀缺问题；(2)构建一个带有几何感知正则化的优化管线，稳定学习过程并确保皮肤权重的平滑性和结构连贯性。此外，该方法还能灵活泛化到3D高斯表示的增强变体，以缓解动画引起的渲染伪影。

Result: 大量实验验证了G-Skin方法的有效性，在多个评价指标上相对现有最先进方法展现出明显优势。

Conclusion: G-Skin通过生成式方法有效解决了3D高斯绑定数据稀缺问题，实现了任意骨架拓扑下3D高斯资产的高表现力、高保真动画绑定，并且具备对增强3D高斯表示的泛化能力。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has achieved remarkable success in photorealistic and efficient rendering, leading to a rapid increase in 3D assets represented by 3D Gaussian primitives. Directly rigging these assets with arbitrary skeleton topologies is highly desirable. However, training a feed-forward skinning framework is infeasible due to the lack of high-quality 3D Gaussian rigging datasets. An alternative solution is to transfer mesh-based techniques to 3D Gaussian-based representation, but 3D Gaussian primitives are not restricted to the surface and lack explicit topological connectivity. Moreover, this kind of method suffers from poor generalization to unseen data due to its strong dependence on training data, while acquiring high-quality rigging data is prohibitively expensive. To address this challenging problem, we propose G-Skin, a novel generative skinning framework designed for expressive and high-fidelity animation with 3D Gaussian representation. To overcome this 3D data scarcity, we introduce a skeleton-controllable image generation model leveraging 2D vision foundation models to distill powerful motion priors into pseudo-guidance. Guided by these priors, we formulate an optimization pipeline incorporating geometry-aware regularizations, which stabilizes the learning process and ensures smooth, structurally coherent skinning weights. G-Skin also generalizes flexibly to the augmented variants of 3D Gaussian representation designed to mitigate animation-induced rendering artifacts. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our approach, demonstrating clear advantages over state-of-the-art methods. Project page: https://yaoyx689.github.io/GSkin.html.

</details>


### [179] [Learning Where to Look and How to Judge: Resolution-agnostic Image Quality Assessment with Quality-aware Saliency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01730)
*Hakan Emre Gedik,Shashank Gupta,Alan Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReLIQS是一个基于CLIP的多尺度补丁驱动的无参考图像质量评估模型，通过预测显著性图选择信息量最丰富的补丁，实现分辨率无关、可跨异构数据集联合训练、计算高效的自适应质量评估，在多种基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NR IQA系统存在多个基本缺陷：激进缩放丢失关键质量线索、无法跨分辨率泛化、难以在MOS尺度不匹配的异构数据集上联合训练、计算开销过大。作者希望通过解决这些问题来推动NR IQA的进一步发展。

Method: ReLIQS采用CLIP视觉骨干编码多分辨率下采样的固定大小补丁（包括原始分辨率），通过轻量级Perceptual Importance Estimator生成IQA特定的重要性图来筛选少量信息丰富的补丁，再由Latent Quality Axis Module聚合补丁嵌入为单一图像级评分。模型具备分辨率无关性、能在多个主观研究数据集上联合训练。

Result: 在真实失真、合成失真和AIGC生成图像等多个基准上，涵盖不同分辨率和失真类型，ReLIQS在泛化性能上优于强CNN、CLIP和MLLM基线，同时计算成本更低或相当。

Conclusion: ReLIQS证明通过多分辨率补丁采样、显著性引导的补丁选择和CLIP特征空间中的质量聚合，可以实现既有高泛化性能又保持计算效率的NR IQA模型，同时解决分辨率适配和异构数据联合训练等核心挑战。

Abstract: No-reference image quality assessment (NR IQA) has recently benefited from deep and multimodal models, yet many SOTA systems still violate at least one basic requirement: they either discard critical quality cues via aggressive resizing, fail to generalize across resolutions, cannot be jointly trained on heterogeneous IQA datasets with mismatched MOS scales, or require prohibitive computation. We present \textbf{ReLIQS}, a model for \textbf{Re}solution-agnostic \textbf{L}earning for \textbf{I}mage \textbf{Q}uality with \textbf{S}aliency, which is resolution-agnostic, preserves original-resolution quality cues, learns from multiple subjective studies, and remains computationally efficient and budget-adaptive. ReLIQS is a CLIP-based multiscale patch-driven architecture that learns both \emph{where to look} and \emph{how to judge} quality. Fixed-size patches are sampled across multiple resolutions, including the original resolution, and encoded with a CLIP vision backbone. A lightweight Perceptual Importance Estimator then predicts IQA-specific importance maps to select a small set of informative patches, and a Latent Quality Axis Module aggregates their embeddings into a single image-level score. Across authentic, synthetic, and AIGC benchmarks spanning diverse resolutions and distortions, ReLIQS generalizes better than strong CNN-, CLIP-, and MLLM-based baselines with matching or reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [180] [IDraw: Artist Verification from Digital Drawing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01737)
*Nayoung Kim,Nan Jiang,Bangjie Sun,Jaewon Shin,Sojeong Kim,Jun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IDraw框架，通过从训练画家的笔触传感器信号中学习绘图行为特征，并抑制画面内容干扰，实现无需传感器数据即可从成品画作进行数字绘画作者验证，将验证错误率降低最多40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字绘画在网络上广泛分享，作者身份验证对保护艺术家和解决纠纷至关重要。但在争议场景中，只能依赖争议画作和参考画作进行验证，面临两大挑战：一是艺术家特有的绘图行为（如笔压、速度）无法从成品画作中获取；二是画面中描绘的物体相似性会掩盖艺术家风格相似性。

Method: 提出IDraw框架，利用配对的数据（画作+平板笔传感器信号）从独立训练艺术家中学习，从而在后期作者争议中仅从成品图像推断绘图行为，无需被验证艺术家的传感器数据。同时通过识别不同艺术家绘制同一物体时共享的内容信息并加以抑制，减少画面内容对验证的干扰。并构建了首个数字绘画作者验证的多模态数据集，包含37位艺术家的1110幅画作和14种平板笔传感器信号。

Result: 在9种图像编码器主干上对未见过的艺术家进行评测，IDraw一致优于基于标准图像的验证方法，将验证错误率最多降低40%。

Conclusion: 从成品图像推断绘图行为并抑制画面内容能够有效提升数字绘画作者身份验证的准确性，展示了该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: As digital drawings are increasingly shared online, reliable authorship verification has become important for protecting artists and resolving disputes. Yet when authorship is questioned, verification may have to rely only on the disputed drawing and reference drawings known to be created by the claimed artist. This setting is challenging for two reasons. First, artist-specific drawing behavior, such as pen pressure and movement speed, is informative but is not available from a completed drawing. Second, similarities in the depicted object or scene can obscure similarities arising from the artist. We propose IDraw, a framework that learns from drawings paired with tablet-pen sensor signals collected from separate training artists. This allows IDraw to infer drawing behavior from completed images during a later authorship dispute, without requiring sensor data from the artist being verified. IDraw also reduces the influence of drawing content by identifying information shared by drawings of the same object across different artists and suppressing it before comparing drawings. To support this approach, we construct the first multimodal dataset for digital drawing authorship verification, containing 1,110 drawings from 37 artists and 14 types of tablet-pen sensor signals. Evaluated on previously unseen artists across nine image-encoder backbones, IDraw consistently outperforms standard image-based verification and reduces verification error by up to 40%. These results demonstrate that inferring drawing behavior from completed images and suppressing drawing content improve digital drawing authorship verification.

</details>


### [181] [SPECTRA: Band-Routed Embedding and Stage-Wise LoRA for Cross-Sensor Fine-Tuning of Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01751)
*Xingyan Li,Jordan A. Caraballo-Vega,Jie Gong,Mark L. Carroll,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPECTRA是一个参数高效的微调框架，用于解决地理空间基础模型（GeoFMs）在适应下游任务时面临的两个挑战：光谱失配（预训练波段与下游传感器波段不一致）和微调成本过高问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GeoFMs在下游任务微调时面临两个主要挑战：(1) 光谱失配问题——预训练模型期望固定波段输入，但下游传感器可能提供不同通道；(2) 微调成本过高。虽然已有工作分别解决这些问题，但如何在光谱失配下联合提升微调性能并降低适应成本仍未被充分探索。

Method: SPECTRA提出两个核心组件：(1) Band-Routed Embedding (BRE)，将所有可用的下游波段映射到预训练GeoFM期望的波段空间，在保持预训练patch embedding接口不变的情况下充分利用所有光谱波段；(2) Stage-wise Transferability-aware LoRA (ST-LoRA)，在微调前估计各阶段的迁移性并为每个阶段分配特定的LoRA秩，将可训练参数集中在目标任务迁移性高的阶段。

Result: 在三个EO预训练GeoFMs和四个下游分割数据集上的实验表明：BRE通过利用所有光谱波段提升了模型性能，ST-LoRA相比全量微调和标准LoRA减少了可训练参数量。

Conclusion: SPECTRA通过联合解决光谱失配和适应成本问题，提供了一种高效的GeoFMs下游适应框架，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models (GeoFMs), pretrained on large-scale geospatial data such as Earth observation (EO), climate, and weather data, have shown promising performance when fine-tuned on diverse downstream tasks. However, there are two challenges of adapting EO-pretrained GeoFMs to practical downstream datasets. The first challenge is how to handle spectral mismatch: pretrained patch embeddings expect a fixed set of input bands, whereas downstream sensors may provide different channels. The second challenge is how to reduce fine-tuning cost and make it efficient. While existing work has made efforts on these challenges individually, jointly improving fine-tuning performance under spectral mismatch while reducing adaptation cost remains underexplored. We propose SPECTRA, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework that addresses both spectral mismatch and adaptation cost. To handle spectral mismatch, SPECTRA introduces Band-Routed Embedding (BRE), which maps all available downstream bands into the band space expected by the pretrained GeoFM. By using BRE, all available bands in the downstream dataset are utilized to improve the selected-band input without changing the pretrained patch embedding interface. To reduce adaptation cost, SPECTRA further introduces a Stage-wise Transferability-aware LoRA (ST-LoRA) fine-tuning. ST-LoRA estimates stage-wise transferability before fine-tuning and assigns stage-specific LoRA ranks, concentrating trainable parameters on the stages with high transferability for the target task. Across three EO-pretrained GeoFMs and four downstream segmentation datasets, experiments show that BRE improves performance by utilizing all spectral bands, while ST-LoRA reduces trainable parameters compared with full fine-tuning and standard LoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/big-data-lab-umbc/SPECTRA.

</details>


### [182] [Can Urban Blight Be Accessed with Vision-language Models: A Case Study in Detroit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01753)
*Xiaohao Yang,Aohua Tian,Derek Van Berkel,Xu Qiang,Mark Lindquist*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于开源大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）和多视角街景的可扩展城市住宅衰败评估框架，通过结构化提示引导模型评估屋顶完整性、墙面损坏和门窗破损等属性，并采用基于XGBoost的集成堆叠方法提升评估精度，结果显示多视角融合和集成学习均能显著提高住宅衰败评估的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统住宅衰败调查因劳动密集和周期长而难以大规模持续维护，亟需一种低成本、可定期更新的系统性评估方法，以支撑城市规划、定向修复和公共健康保障。

Method: 利用开源大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）对多个街景视角进行评估；通过结构化提示词引导模型输出住宅属性的二元判断和概率估计（屋顶完整性、墙面损坏、门窗破损）；采用集成堆叠方法（基于XGBoost）和加权评分系统来综合多个基础模型的结果。

Result: 研究发现：（1）多街景视角能有效提升评估准确性；（2）不同大型视觉语言模型在推理能力上各有优势；（3）集成学习模型优于单个基础模型，在所有住宅条件和衰败评估中均表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为传统衰败调查提供了低成本、可定期更新的补充方案，能够有效支撑住房存量状况的跟踪与管理。

Abstract: Addressing urban blight has seen increased focus in the past 15 years. Assessing urban blight is essential for guiding urban planning, targeting rehabilitation, and safeguarding public health, yet traditional residential blight surveys are difficult to maintain at scale due to the labor-intensive cost and long-term cycle. This study introduced a scalable framework for estimating residential blight using open-source large vision-language models on multiple views. Structured prompts guided models to evaluate housing attributes, including roof integrity, wall damage, and broken or boarded openings, producing both binary assessments and probabilistic estimates of disrepair. To evaluate the performance of these visual assessments, we compared professional human annotations of these features across several models, including an ensemble stacking approach based on XGBoost and a weighted scoring system. Results showed that (i) multiple street views can contribute to the improvement of accuracy, (ii) large vision-language models have different strengths of inference, (iii) the ensemble learner outperforms individual base models, enhancing robustness across all residential conditions and blight assessment. The practical application of the method allows low-cost tracking and management of housing stock conditions, providing a regularly updatable complement to traditional blight surveys.

</details>


### [183] [Pixel Ignores, Superpixel Sees: Adverse Weather Image Restoration via Semantic-Center SSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01760)
*Dayu Li,Shihao Zhou,Leizhi Shu,Jin Wu,Chi Man Vong,Jufeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSR，一种基于语义中心引导的状态空间模型用于恶劣天气图像恢复。通过引入超像素引导的选择性扫描机制（S³M）替代传统的像素序列扫描，并配合区域级门控机制（RGM）进行区域内部校准，在6个基准数据集上取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶劣天气图像恢复方法通过建模像素间关系来解决问题，但这种范式违背了退化在空间上不均匀的事实，且在语义冲突区域学习到缺乏判别力的特征。

Method: 提出SSR框架，核心包含两个模块：（1）超像素引导的选择性扫描机制（S³M），通过超像素聚类将图像划分为感知上一致的区域，并在语义相关区域内进行关系建模；（2）区域级门控机制（RGM），沿通道维度对每个语义超像素单元内的退化离群点进行调制，实现区域内校准。

Result: 在6个公认基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SSR在保持具有竞争力的计算成本的同时，性能优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 将状态空间模型的扫描策略从像素序列转向语义引导方向，能够更有效地处理恶劣天气下空间不均匀的退化问题。

Abstract: Adverse weather image restoration aims to recover clear visibility from degraded images in complex weather conditions.
  Existing works attempt to address this problem by modeling relationships between pixels, however, this paradigm defies the spatially non-uniformity fact of degradations and learns non-discriminative features from semantic-conflict regions.
  In this paper, we propose SSR, a \textbf{S}emantic-center guilded \textbf{S}tate space model for image \textbf{R}estoration.
  The key idea of SSR is to shift the conventional scanning strategy of pixel-serial to semantic-guilded one.
  Specifically, we introduce a Superpixel-guided Selective Scan Mechanism ($\text{S}^3$M), which first partitions the image into perceptually coherent regions via superpixel clustering and then performs relations modeling within the semantic-related regions.
  Moreover, a Region-level Gating Mechanism (RGM) is developed to perform intra-region calibration by modulating degradation outliers within each semantic superpixel unit along the channel dimension.
  Extensive experiments on \textbf{6} well-established benchmarks demonstrate that SSR performs favorably against state-of-the-art models with competitive computational cost.

</details>


### [184] [DecoupleGS: Interactive 3D Gaussian Splatting for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01761)
*Siying Li,Ying Ni,Jie Sun,Jian Sun,Haotian Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DecoupleGS，一种面向大规模端到端自动驾驶评估的解耦式3D高斯泼溅框架，将场景分解为高保真静态背景和可操控动态智能体，通过三个核心模块（感知剪枝与向量量化的资产压缩、语义拓扑引导的地图配准、代理重光照）解决表示冲突，实现实时、高保真且强交互的闭环仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶算法需要在高视觉保真度、强交互性和实时性能兼备的仿真环境中进行严格的闭环验证，但现有方法（从游戏引擎到静态神经渲染）在这三项需求之间存在固有取舍，难以实现端到端测试所需的动态场景组合。

Method: 提出解耦式3DGS框架，将场景分解为静态背景和动态智能体（基于物体中心的标准表示）。引入三个针对性模块：1)通过感知剪枝和向量量化实现资产压缩以支持实时交通渲染；2)利用语义拓扑进行地图引导的几何配准以严格对齐轨迹；3)基于代理的重光照传递环境光照以实现无缝的光度集成。

Result: 大量实验表明DecoupleGS在保真度与效率之间取得了平衡，提升了指标一致性和光度一致性，并提供了一个实用的闭环传感器仿真平台用于端到端自动驾驶评估。

Conclusion: DecoupleGS通过解耦场景表示有效弥合了游戏引擎与神经渲染之间的差距，为端到端自动驾驶评估提供了一种兼顾视觉保真、交互性与实时性的闭环仿真解决方案。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving algorithms require rigorous closed-loop validation in simulation environments offering high visual fidelity, strong interactivity, and real-time performance. Existing approaches, from game engines to static neural rendering, inherently trade off these requirements and struggle with the dynamic scene composition essential for E2E testing. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel decoupled 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) framework tailored for large-scale E2E evaluation. We fundamentally decompose scenes into a high-fidelity static background and manipulable dynamic agents using an object-centric canonical representation. To resolve resulting representational conflicts, we introduce three targeted modules: (1) asset compression via perceptual pruning and vector quantization for real-time traffic rendering; (2) map-guided geometric registration leveraging semantic topology to strictly align trajectories; and (3) proxy-based relighting transferring ambient illumination for seamless photometric integration. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DecoupleGS achieves a balanced fidelity-efficiency trade-off, improves metric and photometric consistency, and provides a practical closed-loop sensor simulation platform for E2E autonomous driving evaluation.

</details>


### [185] [LiveLight: Real-time Streaming Video Relighting with Interactive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01771)
*Yue Ma,Jiangming Wang,Yucheng Wang,Xilai Wang,Zhiyuan Li,Xinyu Wang,Hongyu Liu,Ruofan Liang,Songchun Zhang,Yuxuan Xue,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveLight是首个基于扩散模型的实时流式视频重照明框架，支持交互式3D光照控制，通过轻量适配器注入MPLI光照条件、几何引导反馈分支保障低NFE下的渲染质量，以及渐进式滚动窗口策略实现时序一致的流式交互，在真实与合成基准上达到SOTA质量并保持实时速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频重照明在交互式3D光照控制中至关重要，但面临三大挑战：如何将动态3D光照有效注入扩散模型、如何在极低NFE预算下维持高保真生成以实现实时速度、以及如何支持连续流式交互控制。这些难点尚未被现有工作充分解决。

Method: 提出三个关键设计：(1) 轻量适配器将MPLI（多平面光辐照度）条件——深度感知的辐照度图编码3D光照几何——直接注入扩散主干；(2) 几何引导反馈分支，利用冻结的几何估计器在训练时施加深度与法线一致性约束，确保几何上合理的着色且不增加推理开销；(3) 渐进式滚动窗口策略，维护不同噪声水平的潜变量块去噪阶梯，通过传播中间状态保证时序一致性，支持任意长度的视频重照明与逐帧参考刷新。

Result: 在真实世界与合成基准上的大量实验表明，LiveLight在实现实时速度的同时达到SOTA重照明质量，在时序稳定性、光照可控性和用户偏好方面显著优于离线基线。同时公开模型、训练数据和合成数据生成器以促进该领域研究。

Conclusion: LiveLight验证了扩散模型在低NFE下实现实时流式视频重照明的可行性，通过光照注入、几何约束与流式策略三个维度的协同设计，在质量与速度之间取得了优异的平衡，并为实时交互式重照明研究提供了开源基础设施。

Abstract: We present LiveLight, the first diffusion-based framework for real-time streaming video relighting with interactive 3D lighting control. Achieving this is non-trivial, as it requires overcoming three critical challenges: effectively injecting dynamic 3D lighting into a diffusion model, maintaining high-fidelity generation under an extremely low NFE (Number of Function Evaluations) budget for real-time speed, and facilitating continuous streaming for interactive control. To address these pain points, we propose three key designs. First, for accurate lighting injection, we propose a lightweight adapter that feeds Multi-Plane Light Irradiance (MPLI) conditions-depth-aware irradiance maps encoding 3D lighting geometry-directly into the diffusion backbone. Second, to prevent rendering quality degradation at low NFEs towards real-time distillation, we introduce a geometry-guided feedback branch. This training-time constraint leverages a frozen geometry estimator to enforce depth- and normal-consistent relighting, ensuring geometrically plausible shading without adding inference overhead. Finally, to enable streaming interaction, we develop a progressive rolling-window strategy that maintains a denoising ladder of latent chunks at varying noise levels. By propagating intermediate states, this strategy guarantees temporal coherence and supports arbitrarily long video relighting with per-frame reference refresh. Extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that LiveLight achieves state-of-the-art relighting quality while running at real-time speed, significantly outperforming offline baselines in temporal stability, lighting controllability, and user preference. To foster real-time interactive relighting research, we will publicly release our models, training data, and synthetic data generator.

</details>


### [186] [Investigating Social Bias in Narrative Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01780)
*Junyeong Park,Sowon Min,Euna Jang,Soobin Kim,Jiho Jin,Hyunseung Lim,Gahyeon Bae,Hwajung Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了文本到图像（T2I）模型在不同视觉格式下的社会偏见表现。通过将BBG偏见评估框架适配到图像生成，对比了六种T2I模型在照片、故事板和漫画三种格式中的偏见表达，发现叙事性视觉格式（故事板和漫画）比照片暴露更多偏见，且偏见呈现方式更加明确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型的偏见评估主要集中于照片生成任务，尚未系统研究偏见在叙事性视觉格式（如故事板、漫画）中的表现。随着T2I模型广泛应用于媒体和教育领域，理解不同视觉格式下的偏见表达对模型安全评估至关重要。

Method: 将BBG（基于文本的偏见评估框架）适配到图像生成任务，系统比较六种T2I模型在照片、故事板和漫画三种生成格式中的偏见表现，通过定量统计和定性分析研究偏见的表达方式与程度差异。

Result: 专有模型在照片生成中平均产生25.9%的偏见输出，故事板生成中偏见输出增加9.6个百分点，漫画生成中增加18.2个百分点。照片主要通过微妙的视觉线索编码偏见，而故事板和漫画则通过事件序列、角色定位、叙事结局和文本元素更明确地暴露偏见。

Conclusion: 在照片生成中不够明显的偏见可能在叙事性视觉格式中显现，强调了在照片生成之外，使用多样化视觉格式评估T2I系统偏见的重要性。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation models are increasingly embedded in applications such as media content creation and education, raising concerns about how their outputs may reproduce social biases. Prior work has shown that T2I models exhibit social biases, yet existing evaluations largely focus on a photo generation task. As a result, it remains unclear whether and how such biases manifest in more narrative visual formats, such as storyboards and comics, where characters and events are presented across multiple panels. In this work, we compare bias expression across photo, storyboard, and comic generation in six T2I models by adapting BBG, a text-based bias evaluation framework, to image generation. Our results show that proprietary models generate 25.9% biased outputs in photo generation on average, with biased outputs increasing by 9.6pp in storyboard generation and 18.2pp in comic generation. We also find that photos mainly encode biases through subtle visual cues, while storyboards and comics reveal them more explicitly through event sequencing, character positioning, narrative resolution, and textual elements. These findings show that biases that remain less visible in photo generation may surface in narrative visual formats, highlighting the importance of evaluating T2I systems with diverse visual formats beyond photo generation.

</details>


### [187] [Parameter-Dynamic Adaptive Fusion and Calibration Network for RGBT Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01807)
*Zhaoding Ding,Chenglong Li,Jiandong Jin,Kewei Ying,Wentao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PAFCNet，通过目标自适应超网络动态生成参数，实现多模态融合和时空校准的自适应，提升RGBT跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGBT跟踪器的融合函数使用固定参数，难以适应不同目标和场景的变化；即使动态架构方法选择不同操作，也无法让融合参数随目标状态演化而自适应调整。

Method: 1) 提出目标自适应超网络（TA-HyperNet），利用模板表征生成目标条件参数；2) 设计目标感知参数动态融合模块，用生成参数调制融合过程；3) 提出动态时空校准模块，生成校准参数对时空token进行历史信息校准后再传播，减少噪声累积。

Result: 在多个RGBT跟踪基准上取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过参数动态自适应的融合与校准机制，PAFCNet有效应对了目标外观变化和模态质量波动，提升了RGBT跟踪的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Existing RGBT trackers typically employ fusion functions with fixed parameters across different targets and scenarios. Although dynamic-architecture methods improve fusion flexibility by selecting among predefined operations, they still cannot adapt the fusion parameters to the evolving target state. To address these issues, we propose a Parameter-Dynamic Adaptive Fusion and Calibration Network (PAFCNet) for RGBT tracking. PAFCNet dynamically generates target-conditioned parameters for multimodal fusion and temporal calibration, enabling the tracking process to adapt to target appearance variations and modality quality fluctuations. Specifically, we introduce a Target-Adaptive Hypernetwork (TA-HyperNet) that leverages template representations, which preserve stable target identity and recent appearance changes with less background interference, to generate target-conditioned parameters for subsequent fusion and calibration. Based on TA-HyperNet, we design a target-aware parameter-dynamic fusion module that uses the generated parameters to modulate the fusion process. This enables the fusion module to adapt to changes in target appearance and complex scene conditions. Furthermore, since spatio-temporal information propagation may accumulate tracking noise, we propose a dynamic spatio-temporal calibration module that employs TA-HyperNet to generate calibration parameters for spatio-temporal tokens. By dynamically calibrating historical information before propagation, the module improves the reliability of temporal representations. Experimental results demonstrate that PAFCNet achieves competitive performance on multiple RGBT tracking benchmarks.

</details>


### [188] [SecondOpinion: Anatomy-Aware Gated Reasoning for Efficient Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01808)
*Siam Tahsin Bhuiyan,Rashedur Rahman,Sefatul Wasi,Riyadul Islam,Syoji Kobashi,Ashraful Islam,Saadia Binte Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SecondOpinion框架，通过门控机制（GateKeeper）按需激活解剖引导的第二个流，仅在主流的预测需要额外审查时才启用，在胸片和骨盆骨折数据集上匹配或超越SOTA，同时大幅减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习医学图像模型对所有输入施加固定计算量，忽略了病例难度差异；解剖引导双流架构虽提升诊断性能，但无条件评估两个流，即使在单流即可高置信解决的情况下也浪费计算资源。

Method: 提出SecondOpinon框架：(1)快速主流处理每个病例；(2)GateKeeper作为二分类正确性分类器，判断主流预测是否需要额外审查；(3)激活时通过轻量级交叉注意力融合模块结合两个流；(4)门控信号基于正确性监督而非无监督置信度。

Result: 在统一五类胸片数据集和骨盆骨折数据集上匹配或超过SOTA；解剖引导流仅激活9.23%胸片、可见骨折24.12%、不可见骨折45.71%，激活率与任务难度正相关。

Conclusion: 监督门控信号指向正确性而非依赖无监督置信度，使模型能在真正需要的地方分配解剖推理资源，实现性能与计算效率的平衡。

Abstract: Deep learning models for medical image analysis typically apply a fixed amount of computation to every input, regardless of case difficulty. Anatomy-guided dual-stream architectures have been shown to improve diagnostic performance, but they evaluate both streams unconditionally, even on cases a single stream could already resolve confidently. We propose SecondOpinion, a framework in which a fast primary stream processes every case, while a second, anatomy-guided stream is invoked only when GateKeeper, a gating mechanism trained explicitly as a binary correctness classifier, judges that the primary stream's prediction needs additional scrutiny, much as a clinician might seek a second opinion on a difficult case. When activated, the two streams are combined through a lightweight cross-attention fusion module. We evaluate SecondOpinion on a unified five-class chest X-ray dataset and a pelvic fracture dataset, the latter including a held-out, harder subset of fractures that are invisible on X-ray but confirmed via CT. SecondOpinion matches or exceeds prior state-of-the-art performance on both tasks, while activating its anatomy-guided stream on only 9.23% of chest X-ray cases, rising to 24.12% on visible fractures and 45.71% on invisible fractures, an activation rate that tracks task difficulty directly. These results suggest that supervising a gating signal toward correctness, rather than relying on unsupervised confidence, allows a model to allocate anatomical reasoning where it is actually needed.

</details>


### [189] [DAVET: Denoising-Aware Visual Evidence Trajectory Allocation for Diffusion Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01821)
*Yongkang Zhou,Xiang Xia,Cheng Yan,Fan Xu,Wuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAVET，一种无需训练的框架，通过根据去噪阶段动态分配视觉证据来加速扩散视觉语言模型（dVLMs）的推理，在LLaDA-V和LaViDa上实现1.55倍加速，性能仅下降1.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散视觉语言模型（dVLMs）在每一步去噪时都需要视觉条件输入，导致视觉条件化成为显著的重复推理开销。与自回归解码不同，扩散生成会反复重新访问整个响应，而视觉证据需求随去噪步骤变化很大，现有加速方法（解码端策略或视觉token压缩）没有将视觉证据视为随扩散过程演变的资源。

Method: DAVET是一个无需训练的框架，包含三个关键组件：（1）基于阶段条件的证据轨迹分配策略；（2）利用操作需求设定证据储备，并用轨迹风险调制每一步的证据分配；（3）从单一视觉编码构建层次化的证据视图，将"何时需要多少证据"与"如何构建证据视图"解耦。

Result: 在LLaDA-V和LaViDa两个代表性dVLMs上，跨多个视觉理解基准测试，DAVET实现平均1.55倍加速，平均相对性能下降仅为1.86%。

Conclusion: 去噪感知的视觉证据分配可以有效降低视觉条件化成本，同时大幅保留生成质量，证明了自适应视觉证据分配是加速dVLMs推理的有效方向。

Abstract: Diffusion vision-language models (dVLMs) iteratively denoise masked responses while conditioning each denoising step on visual evidence, making visual conditioning a substantial recurring inference cost. Unlike autoregressive decoding, diffusion generation repeatedly revisits the entire response as uncertainty evolves. Our analysis reveals that visual evidence demand is strongly step-dependent, motivating adaptive allocation across denoising steps. Existing inference acceleration methods operate through decoding-side strategies or visual token compression via pruning and merging, but do not explicitly treat visual evidence as a resource whose demand evolves across the diffusion process. Therefore, we present Denoising-Aware Visual Evidence Trajectory Allocation (DAVET), a training-free framework that allocates visual evidence according to the evolving generation state. Starting from a phase-conditioned evidence trajectory, the proposed allocation policy uses operation demand to set an evidence reserve whose allocation at each denoising step is modulated by trajectory risk. DAVET realizes the resulting budgets through a hierarchy of evidence views constructed from a single visual encoding, separating when and how much evidence is needed from how the evidence views are constructed. Evaluated on two representative dVLMs, LLaDA-V and LaViDa, across multiple visual-understanding benchmarks, DAVET achieves an average speedup of 1.55$\times$ with an average relative performance drop of 1.86\%, showing that denoising-aware visual evidence allocation can reduce visual conditioning cost while largely preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [190] [Detail Continuation over a Trustworthy Coarse Scale for Autoregressive Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01823)
*Hongyi Fang,Jiahui Wu,Yichen Yue,Benjia Zhou,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出K2N方法，将视觉自回归（VAR）超分辨率从传统的全路径1到N生成重构成k到N的细节延续过程：早期粗尺度状态直接从低分辨率输入建立，仅对剩余细尺度进行自回归恢复，以减少长自回归链的误差累积并缓解生成超分辨率中的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成超分辨率（GSR）中幻觉问题持续存在，现有方法对可靠粗尺度信息与不确定细尺度细节的划分往往是隐式的。VAR建模的粗到细逐尺度预测提供了显式的尺度级生成接口，但现有VAR-based SR方法仍继承完整的1到N自回归路径，粗尺度信息本应更可靠，而长自回归链容易累积预测误差。

Method: K2N将VAR-based SR从全路径生成重构为k到N的细节延续过程：早期粗尺度状态直接从LR输入建立（不经过自回归），仅剩余更细的尺度通过自回归方式恢复，从而缩短自回归链长度并利用LR中相对可靠的粗尺度信息。

Result: K2N在标准SR指标上与VARSR基线保持竞争力，同时在以幻觉为重点的评估上展现出更明显的优势。

Conclusion: 显式地从尺度角度重新思考生成路径是提升生成超分辨率可靠性的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a persistent challenge in generative super-resolution (GSR), where reconstructed results may contain visually plausible yet weakly supported content, structural deviations, or unnatural textures with respect to the low-resolution (LR) input. Existing GSR methods have extensively explored the trade-off between perceptual realism and reconstruction fidelity, but the division between preserving reliable coarse-scale information and restoring more uncertain fine details is often handled implicitly within the overall restoration process. Visual autoregressive (VAR) modeling provides a natural opportunity to revisit this issue, as its coarse-to-fine next-scale prediction offers an explicit scale-wise generation interface. However, existing VAR-based SR methods still inherit the original full 1-to-$N$ autoregressive generation path, even though, for super-resolution, coarse-scale information in LR is often relatively more reliable, while long autoregressive chains may accumulate prediction errors. Motivated by these observations, we propose \textbf{K2N}, which reformulates VAR-based SR from full-path generation into a $k$-to-$N$ detail continuation process. Specifically, early coarse-scale states are established directly from LR, while only the remaining finer scales are restored autoregressively. Experimental results show that K2N remains competitive with the VARSR baseline on standard SR metrics, while exhibiting clearer advantages on hallucination-focused evaluation. These findings suggest that explicitly rethinking the generation path in a scale-wise manner can be a promising direction for improving the reliability of generative super-resolution. Our code will be released soon at https://github.com/BRL-SYSU/K2NSR.

</details>


### [191] [PartMat: Material-Aware 3D Part Decomposition with a Single Global Latent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01825)
*Guangming Fu,Jin Song,Yiyun Fei,Guoqiu Li,Ruigao Yang,Jianan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PartMat提出了一种高效的材质感知3D部件分解流水线，通过单一全局潜在表征表示多部件几何，利用PartVAE一次前向解码所有材质部件，并结合扩散模型和稀疏体素流匹配模型实现精确的材质分配与几何细节恢复，在解耦推理成本的同时显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D部件级生成方法通常按功能语义而非实际应用（如室内设计）所需的可编辑材质边界（如织物、木材、金属）来分解物体，且现有方法独立生成各部件，导致计算成本随部件数量线性增长。

Method: （1）提出PartVAE学习统一表征，在单次前向传播中解码所有材质部件，将推理成本与部件数量解耦；（2）基于该表征训练扩散模型进行部件生成，并通过强化学习优化材质分配和重叠抑制；（3）引入带部件注意力的稀疏体素流匹配模型进行几何后处理以恢复细粒度几何细节。

Result: 大量实验表明PartMat在材质感知分解精度上显著优于现有基线，同时达到可比的几何质量，并保持高效推理性能。

Conclusion: PartMat通过全局潜在表征和单次前向解码策略，有效解决了现有方法在材质边界分解和推理效率方面的局限，为实用3D应用中的结构化可编辑资产生成提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Part-level 3D generation has recently attracted increasing attention for producing structured and editable 3D assets. However, existing methods typically decompose objects according to functional semantics rather than the editable material boundaries (e.g., fabric, wood, metal) required in practical 3D applications such as interior design. Additionally, current methods often generate parts independently, causing computational costs to scale linearly with the part count. To address these limitations, we present PartMat, an efficient material-aware 3D part decomposition pipeline that represents multi-part geometry with a single global latent. Given a reference image and a single whole-object geometry, PartMat decomposes the object into parts that follow material boundaries. First, we propose PartVAE to learn such a unified representation and decode all material parts in a single forward pass, thereby decoupling inference cost from the number of parts. Second, with this representation, a diffusion model is trained for part generation and refined via reinforcement learning for accurate material assignment and overlap suppression. Finally, to recover fine-grained geometric details, we introduce a sparse-voxel flow-matching model with part attention for geometry post-processing. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PartMat significantly outperforms existing baselines in material-aware decomposition accuracy and achieves comparable geometric quality, while maintaining efficient inference.

</details>


### [192] [DeepVoyager-VL: Incentivizing Vision-in-the-Loop Search for Long-Horizon Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01827)
*Huanyao Zhang,Jiepeng Zhou,Runhao Zhao,Yanzhe Shan,Jiaoyang Chen,Bowen Zhou,Bo Li,Fang Wang,Jialong Wu,Zhengwei Tao,Lang Mei,Xiaohan Yu,Liyan Liu,Chong Chen,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepVoyager-VL，一个长时程多模态深度搜索框架，通过将视觉证据融入中间推理环节（而非仅限输入或答案阶段），实现视觉驱动的持续检索，并在十个多模态搜索基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs的静态参数知识难以应对知识密集型、动态演化的开放世界问题。多模态深度搜索虽已从单轮事实检索向长时程多轮搜索演进，但现有方法通常将视觉仅用于输入或答案阶段，忽视了其在中间推理中的作用，且缺乏针对长时程交互的设计，导致视觉证据难以驱动持续检索，限制了交互深度和推理跨度。

Method: 1) 构建多模态事件图驱动数据合成，生成具有中间视觉依赖和长推理链的问题；2) 设计智能体框架，支持主动视觉获取和按需图像加载；3) 在合成数据上进行微调（无需强化学习）。

Result: 在十个多模态搜索基准上的大量实验表明，该方法有效。

Conclusion: DeepVoyager-VL通过视觉在环（vision-in-the-loop）机制，使视觉证据能够驱动中间推理阶段的持续检索，克服了现有方法将视觉局限在输入或答案阶段的不足，显著提升了长时程多模态深度搜索能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced visual understanding and reasoning, yet their static parametric knowledge limits their ability to address knowledge-intensive and dynamically evolving open-world problems. To move beyond this limitation, multimodal deep search has emerged as a key direction for open-world information access, evolving from single-turn factual retrieval toward long-horizon, multi-turn search guided by visual evidence. However, existing methods typically confine vision to the input or answer stage, overlooking its role in intermediate reasoning, and lack designs tailored to long-horizon interaction. Consequently, visual evidence rarely drives continued retrieval, constraining both interaction depth and reasoning span. To address these limitations, we propose DeepVoyager-VL, a long-horizon multimodal deep-search framework for vision-in-the-loop search. Specifically, we construct a multimodal event graph to drive data synthesis, yielding problems with intermediate visual dependencies and long reasoning chains. We then design an agent framework for active visual acquisition and on-demand image loading. Finally, we fine-tune models on the synthesized data without reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments across ten multimodal search benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [193] [MoCRA: Mixture of Compositional Rank-1 Atoms for 4K All-in-One Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01829)
*Yongcong Wang,Pu Wang,Hingchin Chen,Runci Bai,Yucheng Xin,Chen Wu,Chengchao Shen,Guangwei Gao,Siyuan Yao,Pengwen Dai,Zhuoran Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoCRA，一个无需退化标签、支持原生4K输出且播放稳定的统一视频恢复模型。作者构建了UHV-4K-AIO基准数据集，在同一100个4K干净片段上合成物理建模的雾、雨、噪声和低光退化，并发现雾与低光在降采样后仍存留，而雨和噪声仅存在于原生尺度。基于此，MoCRA采用频带匹配的组合条件化设计——一个字典的秩1原子按帧稀疏重组，同时条件化每个片段一次粗分支和浅层原生分辨率精化器，仅3.6M参数且无需光流。模型训练一次即可处理四种任务，在任务平均PSNR上超过十一个重训练基线，保持与基于光流模型相当的扭曲误差，恢复原生4K仅需不到0.5秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署的视频恢复系统需同时满足三个需求：无需退化标签、原生4K输出、播放稳定性。现有方法分别解决各单一问题，但在组合场景下失效——逐帧退化预测在帧间抖动、降采样代理丢失雨和噪声、密集时间对齐不适合4K内存，且缺乏同时涵盖这些挑战的配对基准。

Method: 1) 构建UHV-4K-AIO基准：在100个4K干净片段上基于共享深度和运动，物理建模渲染雾、雨、传感器噪声和低光。2) 提出MoCRA架构：频带匹配的组合条件化——雾和低光在降采样带处理，雨和噪声在原生尺度处理。一个秩1原子字典按帧稀疏重组，同时条件化一次每片段粗分支与浅层原生精化器，仅3.6M参数，无需光流。

Result: MoCRA在四个任务的联合恢复中取得最佳任务平均PSNR，超越十一个重新训练的图像和视频基线；扭曲误差与基于光流的视频模型相当但从不估计运动；原生4K恢复耗时不到0.5秒，而最快的基线需1.7秒。

Conclusion: 通过揭示不同退化类型在尺度上的不对称分布特性（雾/低光可降采样处理，雨/噪声必须原生尺度处理），MoCRA以极轻量的参数和极快的推理速度实现了统一的多退化4K视频恢复，同时保持与基于光流的模型相当的时序稳定性，为实际场景部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-world video arrives hazy, rainy, dark, or noisy, and a deployable restorer faces three demands at once: no degradation label, native 4K output, and stability in playback. Existing methods answer them separately and break on the joint problem, because per-frame degradation readings flip between frames, downsampled proxies erase the rain and noise they are meant to remove, and dense temporal alignment does not fit 4K memory. No paired benchmark even poses that problem, so we build one. UHV-4K-AIO renders physically modeled haze, rain, sensor noise, and low light over the same 100 clean 4K clips with shared depth and motion, and its construction exposes the split MoCRA is built on: haze and low light survive aggressive downsampling, while rain and noise exist only at native scale. Band-matched compositional conditioning follows, spending conditioning capacity, computation, and supervision in the band where each degradation lives. One dictionary of rank-1 atoms, recomposed sparsely per frame, conditions both a once-per-clip coarse branch and a shallow native-resolution refiner, in 3.6M parameters and with no optical flow. Trained once for all four tasks, MoCRA takes the best task-mean PSNR of eleven retrained image and video baselines, holds warping error at the level of the flow-based video models while never estimating motion, and restores native 4K in under half a second, against 1.7 seconds for the fastest baseline.

</details>


### [194] [Decoupling semantics from vision: A framework for faithful visual-text compression evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01848)
*Yonghan Gao,Zehong Chen,Lijian Xu,Jingzhi Chen,Jingwei Guan,Xingyu Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个新的评估框架，通过解耦MLLM的语言先验能力来更准确地评估视觉文本压缩（VTC）质量，并引入ZeroSense Benchmark确保测试样本低语义相关性，实验表明VTC质量与下游任务准确率存在显著分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VTC评估协议过度依赖下游任务性能，由于MLLM本身具有强大的语言先验能力，这种评估指标无法准确衡量文本保留质量，需要解耦的评估框架。

Method: 提出了一个解耦MLLM能力的评估框架，并引入ZeroSense Benchmark，通过消除测试样本的文本依赖性和确保低语义相关性，使评估结果纯粹反映VTC质量而不受下游模型语义推理能力的影响。

Result: 在多组数据集上的大量实验表明，VTC质量与下游任务准确率之间存在显著分歧，验证了解耦评估框架的必要性。

Conclusion: 现有评估方式会高估VTC的真实质量，需要解耦评估框架来忠实反映VTC的文本保留能力。

Abstract: Recent visual-text compression (VTC) methods, typified by DeepSeek-OCR, report impressive high token compression ratios for long-context modeling tasks by leveraging text-to-image rendering. However, existing evaluation protocols heavily rely on downstream task performance. Such evaluation metrics fail to accurately measure text preservation due to the strong inherent linguistic priors of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). In this work, we introduce a new evaluation framework that decouples MLLMs' capabilities to faithfully assess VTC quality. Within this framework, we further introduce the ZeroSense Benchmark to ensure low semantic correlation of testing samples. By eliminating textual dependencies, our benchmark guarantees that the evaluation results are purely reflective of VTC quality, unaffected by the semantic inference capabilities of downstream models. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that VTC quality and downstream task accuracy diverge significantly, highlighting the necessity of our decoupled evaluation framework.

</details>


### [195] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-III: Semantic Geometry Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01876)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TGO-III框架，通过多种观测手段（线性探针精度、Fisher比率、类质心距离、局部本征维数、局部PCA秩）系统分析ViT-Small/16在训练过程中语义几何和类可分性的演变，验证了语义扩展假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Vision Transformer已被广泛采用，但现有研究多聚焦于token几何和训练动力学，对表征协方差结构和类级几何组织的演变探索不足，需要更系统的分析框架。

Method: TGO-III框架采用多种互补的观测手段：线性探针精度、Fisher比率、类质心距离、局部本征维数和局部PCA秩，从多个维度量化判别性表征的渐进演化。

Result: 分析发现：类表征逐步变得更线性可分，Fisher判别性增强，类质心间距增大，局部表征流形表现出结构化、类依赖的几何复杂度。这些证据支持语义扩展假说。

Conclusion: TGO-III扩展了Transformer几何观测站框架，建立了流形几何、协方差演变与语义组织之间的直接联系，表明流形扩展伴随表征向越来越具有判别性的语义结构渐进组织。

Abstract: With the widespread adoption of Vision Transformers in modern AI, the need to analyze their inherent representational behavior has become increasingly important. While most existing studies emphasize token geometries and training dynamics, the evolution of representational covariance structures and class-level geometric organization remains comparatively underexplored. In this work, we investigate semantic geometry and class separability as representations evolve across the layers of ViT-Small/16 through TGO-III: Semantic Geometry Observatory. It is a framework designed to analyze the emergence of semantic organization, feature evolution, and class-wise representation geometry throughout training. The framework employs multiple complementary observatories, including Linear Probe Accuracy, Fisher Ratio, Class Centroid Distances, Local Intrinsic Dimension, and Local PCA Rank, to quantify the progressive evolution of discriminative representations. Our analysis reveals that class representations become progressively more linearly separable, Fisher discriminability increases, class centroids move farther apart, and local representation manifolds exhibit structured class-dependent geometric complexity. These observations provide empirical evidence supporting the Semantic Expansion Hypothesis, suggesting that the manifold expansion observed in previous observatories is accompanied by the progressive organization of representations into increasingly discriminative semantic structures. Collectively, TGO-III extends the Transformer Geometry Observatory framework by establishing a direct connection between manifold geometry, covariance evolution, and semantic organization during Transformer training.

</details>


### [196] [Beyond Illumination: A Conditional Mutual Information-Guided Network for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01886)
*Ya-nan Guan,Shaonan Zhang,Tao Dai,Tianqu Zhuang,Yongchao Qiao,Zhensen Chen,Shu-Tao Xia,Hang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CMIG-Net，一种基于条件互信息的低光图像增强网络，通过CMIC模块和D2IR模块实现亮度与色度分支间的自适应信息交互，在多个基准上超越CIDNet，PSNR最高提升0.619 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双分支LLIE方法（如CIDNet）在HVI色彩空间中分别建模色度和亮度信息，但忽略了两个分量之间的相互交互，限制了表征能力并导致次优的增强效果。

Method: 提出CMIG-Net，包含两个核心模块：(1) 条件互信息校准(CMIC)模块，生成条件信息图以实现区域自适应的色度表征重校准；(2) 动态双分支信息恢复(D2IR)模块，根据条件先验和瞬时恢复状态自适应控制亮度与色度分支间的双向信息流。

Result: 在配对LLIE基准上优于CIDNet，PSNR最高提升0.619 dB，在具有挑战性的Sony-Total-Dark数据集上提升了0.382 dB。

Conclusion: 以条件互信息作为度量来引导亮度与色度之间的信息交互，能够有效提升低光图像增强的性能，验证了分支间交互建模的重要性。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) seeks to restore structural fidelity, natural color rendition, and proper exposure from images captured under inadequate lighting conditions. Recent state-of-the-art approaches, such as CIDNet, adopt a dual-branch architecture comprising a chrominance (HV) branch and an intensity (I) branch to separately model decoupled chromatic and luminance information within the HVI color space. However, these methods overlook the mutual interaction between intensity and chrominance components, which inherently limits their representational capacity and leads to suboptimal enhancement performance. To address this limitation, we propose the Conditional Mutual Information-Guided Network (CMIG-Net), which leverages conditional mutual information as a principled metric to quantitatively assess the contribution of chrominance features conditioned on the available intensity information. In particular, we design a Conditional Mutual Information Calibration (CMIC) module that generates a conditional information map, enabling region-adaptive recalibration of chrominance representations according to local illumination statistics. Furthermore, we introduce a Dynamic Dual-branch Information Restoration (D2IR) module, which adaptively governs bidirectional information flow between the intensity and chrominance branches, guided by both the conditional prior and the instantaneous restoration state. Extensive experiments on paired LLIE benchmarks demonstrate that CMIG-Net consistently outperforms CIDNet, achieving up to a 0.619 dB gain in PSNR, with a 0.382 dB improvement specifically on the challenging Sony-Total-Dark dataset.

</details>


### [197] [GeoCore-9B: Towards Geo-Aware Generative Foundation Models in Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01896)
*Jeonghyeok Do,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCore-9B是一个9B参数量的EO生成基础模型，是全球首个完全从零开始在纯EO数据上训练的此类规模模型，基于Flow Matching的Diffusion Transformer架构，支持文本和连续地理元数据条件生成，并通过Geospatial Semantic Alignment损失克服训练挑战，在可视保真度和地理结构准确性上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EO生成模型依赖微调自然图像预训练模型，存在可扩展性受限和视角偏差与地理空间约束冲突的问题。

Method: 基于Flow Matching的Diffusion Transformer（DiT）架构，从零训练于9B参数规模；原生支持文本描述和连续地理元数据（地面采样距离、纬度、经度）条件生成；提出Geospatial Semantic Alignment损失，从冻结的专家教师网络蒸馏地表结构先验，约束扩散潜变量训练轨迹且不增加推理开销；在Git-10M全球数据集上预训练。

Result: 在标准代理生成任务以及云去除、SAR到光学跨模态翻译等实际EO应用中均表现出色，在可视保真度和地理结构准确性上达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: GeoCore-9B证明了从零训练大规模纯EO生成基础模型的可行性，通过地理语义对齐有效解决了尺度训练中的收敛与空间定向挑战，为EO生成模型提供了超越自然图像微调范式的可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Existing generative models for earth observation (EO) predominantly rely on fine-tuning natural image priors, which limits their scalability and introduces perspective biases that conflict with geospatial constraints. To address this, we introduce GeoCore-9B, a 9-billion-parameter generative foundation model, which is the first of its scale to be trained from scratch exclusively on EO data. Unlike previous EO foundation models, GeoCore-9B is built upon a Flow Matching-based Diffusion Transformer (DiT) and natively conditions generation on text descriptions and continuous geospatial metadata, including ground sample distances, latitudes, and longitudes. To overcome the convergence and spatial disorientation challenges of training at this scale, we propose a Geospatial Semantic Alignment loss. This objective distills structural Earth surface priors (e.g., terrain and urban areas) from a frozen specialist teacher network, constraining the diffusion latent trajectory during training without adding inference overhead. Pre-trained on the global-scale Git-10M dataset, GeoCore-9B demonstrates strong downstream versatility. Beyond standard proxy generative tasks, we show that GeoCore-9B can be effectively adapted for practical EO applications, including highly challenging tasks such as cloud removal and SAR-to-optical cross-modal translation. Extensive evaluations confirm that GeoCore-9B establishes new state-of-the-art performance in both visual fidelity and geographic structural accuracy.

</details>


### [198] [PhotoHOI: Synthesizing 3D Hand-Object Interactions from a Single RGB Photograph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01905)
*Zhenhao Zhang,Jiajun Zhang,Wei Min,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhotoHOI从单张RGB照片和开放词汇语言指令出发，合成3D手物交互序列，无需预定义物体几何或轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手物交互（HOI）合成方法通常需要预定义的物体几何、物体轨迹或特定任务条件，限制了其在真实世界自然输入下的应用。

Method: 首先使用视觉语言模型将输入图像和指令解析为结构化任务规范（交互对象、目标区域、空间关系），然后恢复紧凑的任务相关3D场景并规划平滑的碰撞感知物体轨迹；通过学习可迁移的任务条件接触先验和接触条件抓取先验来合成手部运动，并在学习的潜在空间中进行抓取细化以约束到手部姿态流形。

Result: 在GRAB和H2O数据集上相比代表性基线方法改善了接触质量并减少了穿透；在真实照片上展示了更高的任务成功率和场景一致性，以及对未见物体和开放词汇指令的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PhotoHOI首次实现了仅凭单张照片和自然语言指令即可合成3D手物交互序列，突破了传统方法对预定义物体条件的依赖，具有良好的真实世界泛化能力。

Abstract: Hand-object interaction (HOI) is a fundamental human behavior with broad applications in AR/VR, digital humans, and embodied interaction. Existing methods typically require predefined object geometry, object trajectories, or task-specific conditions, limiting their use with natural real-world inputs. To address this, we study a more practical problem of synthesizing 3D hand-object interaction sequences from a single RGB photograph and an open-vocabulary language instruction, and introduce PhotoHOI. PhotoHOI first uses a vision-language model to parse the input image and instruction into a structured task specification, including the interaction object, target region, and spatial relation. It then recovers a compact task-relevant 3D scene and plans a smooth collision-aware object trajectory based on the recovered object states, support relations, and surrounding scene geometry. To synthesize hand motion that generalizes to real-world photographs and unseen objects, it learns transferable task-conditioned contact and contact-conditioned grasp priors from large-scale affordance and HOI data. The grasp is further refined in a learned latent space, constraining the optimization to a plausible hand-pose manifold. Experiments on GRAB and H2O demonstrate improved contact quality and reduced penetration over representative baselines. Results on real-world photographs further demonstrate higher task success and scene consistency, together with generalization to unseen objects and open-vocabulary instructions.

</details>


### [199] [Assessing the Benefits of Combining Advanced Deep Learning Techniques for Post-Disaster Building Damage Assessment from UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01906)
*Huy Quang Ung,Guillaume Habault,Roberto Legaspi,Hao Niu,Lian Cao,Masato Taya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合框架，将CV模型的建筑检测与LVLMs的损伤评估解耦结合，在RescueNet和FloodNet基准上优于基线模型，仅需有限的标注数据即可准确统计不同损坏程度的建筑数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后建筑损伤快速评估至关重要但充满挑战。现有CV模型需要大量标注数据、跨区域泛化能力差且局限于特定任务；而LVLMs虽然具备强推理和泛化能力，但在精确的底层感知任务（如目标检测和边界框生成）上表现不佳，且微调需要大量数据。两者各有优劣，需要取长补短。

Method: 提出一个混合框架，将检测与损伤评估解耦：首先使用CV模型检测建筑并生成边界框，然后将这些边界框传递给LVLM进行损伤分类和上下文解释。在检测阶段仅需有限的标注数据。

Result: 在RescueNet和FloodNet两个真实基准上评估，最佳组合准确统计了完好、部分损坏和完全摧毁的建筑数量，相比孤立基线提升了最多2.1个R²分数点。此外提供了失败场景和边缘案例的详细分析。

Conclusion: 混合框架通过解耦检测和评估任务，有效结合了CV模型的精确性与LVLMs的推理能力，在有限标注数据条件下实现了更优的灾后建筑损伤评估性能，并为从业者提供了实践见解和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Rapid and accurate post-disaster building damage assessment is essential, yet remains a challenging task. Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) imagery offers a timely and high-resolution view of affected areas, but existing Computer Vision (CV) models often demand large annotated datasets, generalize poorly across geographic regions and their assessment policies, and are confined to the specific tasks they were trained for. Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) offer a promising alternative through their strong reasoning and generalization capabilities, but fall short on precise, low-level perception tasks such as object detection and accurate bounding box generation. Furthermore, they often require a substantial amount of data for effective fine-tuning on domain-specific tasks. In this paper, we propose a hybrid framework that decouples detection from damage assessment, combining the precision of CV models with the reasoning power of LVLMs. A CV model first detects buildings and generates bounding boxes on the image that are then passed to an LVLM for damage classification and contextual interpretation. We evaluated our framework on two real-world benchmarks: RescueNet and FloodNet. In particular, the best combination under this framework accurately counts intact, partially damaged and completely destroyed buildings, surpassing isolated baselines by up to 2.1 R^2 points, while requiring only limited annotated data for the detection stage. Beyond reporting aggregate gains, we provide a detailed analysis of failure scenarios and edge cases, offering practical insights for practitioners and concrete directions for future work. Our source code and data are publicly available to the research community via the following repository: https://github.com/ungquanghuy-kddi/VLM_GDINO.git

</details>


### [200] [PNEC-Mamba: Prototype-Guided Positive-Negative Evidence Calibration for Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01910)
*Mingzhen Xu,Can Xu,Di Wang,Haonan Guo,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PNEC-Mamba框架，从像素级证据可靠性建模的角度解决高光谱图像分类中像素表征模糊的问题。该框架通过原型引导的正负证据校准，分离判别性证据与干扰信息，估计像素级可靠性并进行选择性校准，在三个基准数据集上取得了优于现有方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像中像素表征常因光谱相似性、混合像元及局部上下文干扰而模糊不清，现有方法多聚焦于学习更强表征或建模更广上下文，却很少探究学习到的表征是否为分类决策提供了可靠证据或引入了干扰信息。

Method: 提出PNEC-Mamba原型引导正负证据校准框架：(1) 全图像状态空间编码器提取像素表征，动态类原型提供同步演化的语义参考；(2) 通过像素-原型竞争推导正负证据，显式分离支持分类的判别线索与竞争类别的混淆信号；(3) 基于证据关系引入多源不确定性估计策略，评估像素级可靠性，对不确定区域进行更强的证据校准；(4) 应用全分辨率一致性精化步骤恢复局部空间细节，改善预测边界的连贯性。

Result: 在三个基准高光谱数据集上的大量实验表明，PNEC-Mamba相比现有最先进方法取得了更优的分类性能。

Conclusion: 从像素级证据可靠性建模角度出发，通过正负证据分离与选择性校准，有效解决了高光谱图像分类中表征模糊带来的干扰问题，验证了证据可靠性建模在高光谱图像分类中的有效性。

Abstract: In real-world hyperspectral scenes, pixel representations are often ambiguous due to factors such as spectral similarity, mixed pixels, and local context interference, which may simultaneously encode discriminative evidence and interfering information. Existing methods mainly focus on learning more powerful representations or modeling broader contexts, but rarely investigate whether the learned representations provide reliable evidence or introduce interference into classification decisions. To address this issue, we view hyperspectral image classification from the perspective of pixel-level evidence reliability modeling and propose PNEC-Mamba, a prototype-guided positive-negative evidence calibration framework. The framework progressively establishes semantic references, separates class-related evidence from interference, estimates pixel-level reliability, and performs selective calibration. First, a full-image state-space encoder extracts pixel representations, while dynamic class prototypes provide semantic references that evolve jointly with the feature space. Subsequently, positive and negative evidence is derived from pixel-prototype competition, explicitly separating discriminative cues that support classification from confusing signals associated with competing classes. Based on these evidence relationships, a multi-source uncertainty estimation strategy is introduced to assess pixel-level reliability, enabling stronger evidence calibration for uncertain regions. Finally, a full-resolution consistency refinement step is applied to recover local spatial details and improve boundary coherence in the final predictions. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that PNEC-Mamba achieves superior classification performance compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [201] [CHOW-SLAM: Compact Hybrid Representation with Complementary Overlap Window Optimization for RGB-D SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01914)
*Wenxuan Ji,Jin Xiao,Xiaoguang Hu,Jiaqi Shi,Zichong Jia,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHOW-SLAM是一个基于NeRF的密集RGB-D SLAM框架，通过显式构建互补的空间约束（紧凑参数哈希混合表示）和时间约束（互补重叠窗口策略），在有限在线资源下实现了优于现有方法的重建质量和相机跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于NeRF的SLAM系统在有限在线资源下，难以同时构建两类约束：来自场景表示的紧凑且具判别性的空间约束，以及来自历史观测的持久时间约束，这限制了重建和跟踪的性能。

Method: 提出CHOW-SLAM框架：(1) 空间上采用参数哈希(P-H)混合表示，按平面和网格跨尺度组织组件，配合统一多输出解码器对齐TSDF和密度诱导的光线终止分布；(2) 时间上采用互补重叠窗口策略，在固定预算内保留近期帧、选择高重叠局部帧并引入时间分布的历史关键帧；(3) 采用损失感知的关键帧插入和束调整调度；(4) 使用ORB跟踪与几何位姿估计进行位姿初始化，再通过神经渲染优化提升跟踪稳定性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，CHOW-SLAM在场景重建质量和相机跟踪精度上均优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式构建互补的空间和时间约束，CHOW-SLAM有效解决了有限资源下NeRF-SLAM的约束构建难题，实现了更优的重建与跟踪性能，且源代码已开源。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) based on Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) enables dense, continuous scene reconstruction. However, existing systems operating with limited online resources struggle to simultaneously construct two types of constraints, namely, compact yet discriminative spatial constraints derived from scene representations and persistent temporal constraints derived from historical observations. To address this challenge, we propose CHOW-SLAM, a dense RGB-D SLAM framework that explicitly constructs these complementary spatial and temporal constraints. Spatially, we propose a compact parametric-hash (P-H) hybrid representation that organizes components based on planes and grids across scales in P and H branches. A unified multi-output decoder further aligns the ray termination distributions induced by TSDF and density, preserving geometry and appearance under a compact parameter budget. Temporally, we propose a complementary overlap-window strategy to prevent optimization from being dominated by short-term overlap or weakly related historical observations. Within a fixed budget, the strategy retains recent frames, selects high-overlap local frames, and introduces temporally distributed historical keyframes. Loss-aware keyframe insertion and bundle adjustment scheduling further adapt optimization to tracking quality. In addition, ORB-based tracking and geometric pose estimation are used for pose initialization, followed by neural rendering optimization to improve tracking stability. Extensive evaluations on multiple datasets demonstrate that CHOW-SLAM outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both scene reconstruction quality and camera tracking accuracy. The source code is available at https://github.com/jinjidexiaohuoban/CHOW-SLAM.

</details>


### [202] [Recompute or Reuse? Diagnosing and Mitigating Textual Shortcuts in VLM Self-Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01930)
*Wenxiao Fan,Jingling Fu,Fang Li,Luohang Liu,Yu He,Lichen Ma,Zhiyang Yu,Weishan Bi,Junshi Huang,Yan Li,Gu Simiu,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型（VLMs）在视觉证据变化时，会复用先前思维链（CoT）中的证据性文本内容作为"捷径"，而非从当前图像重新计算。作者提出一种无需训练的干预方法FSAF（Fresh-State Attention Firewall），通过隔离新鲜视觉计算与旧文本推理，将视觉更新率从35.28%提升至53.61%，并将先验答案率从39.22%降至3.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM被期望在视觉证据变化时修正推理，但失败常被归因于视觉注意力不足或上下文惯性，缺乏对"模型实际复用什么而非重新计算什么"的清晰理解。本文旨在揭示先验CoT中证据性内容如何形成文本捷径，与视觉重新计算产生行为竞争。

Method: 在16个VLM上进行匹配的反事实分析：移除/重排先验CoT中的证据性内容、非证据上下文和最终答案片段，比较对答案偏好的影响程度。基于此发现设计FSAF干预，通过隔离新鲜计算与先验CoT来阻断文本捷径，并在5个VLM上验证效果。

Result: 证据性内容是先验CoT影响的最稳健载体；移除证据性内容比移除等长非证据上下文或最终答案更能改变答案偏好；打乱证据顺序也会削弱先验控制。在答案修正后捷径仍有残余影响。FSAF将视觉更新率从35.28%提升至53.61%，先验答案率从39.22%降至3.67%。

Conclusion: 可靠的VLM自我反思不仅需要"重新看"，更需要保护新鲜视觉计算免受陈旧文本复用的干扰。FSAF作为一种无需训练的有效干预，证明了隔离策略在抑制文本捷径方面的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are expected to revise their reasoning when visual evidence changes. Failures to do so are often attributed to insufficient visual attention or contextual inertia, leaving unclear what models reuse instead of recomputing from the current image. We show that evidence-bearing reasoning in a prior chain of thought (CoT) can form a textual shortcut that competes behaviorally with visual recomputation. Across 16 VLMs, a matched counterfactual analysis identifies evidence-bearing content as the most robust carrier of prior-CoT influence. Removing this evidence-bearing content shifts answer preference more than removing length-matched non-evidence context or the final-answer span, with prior control weakening progressively as more stale evidence is removed. Reordering this evidence also weakens prior control, showing that its organization modulates shortcut strength. Beyond the immediate answer, the shortcut can retain residual influence after answer correction: weakening current-image support shifts preference back toward the prior answer, while repeated prior answers and reused premises arise mainly when the shortcut remains active. To limit this influence, we introduce Fresh-State Attention Firewall (FSAF), a training-free intervention that isolates fresh computation from the prior CoT. Across five VLMs, FSAF raises visual update rate from 35.28% to 53.61% and reduces prior-answer rate from 39.22% to 3.67%. Reliable VLM self-reflection therefore requires more than looking again: fresh visual recomputation must be protected from stale textual reuse.

</details>


### [203] [UniMoCa: Unifying Motion and Camera Controls as Visual Proxies for Faithful Human Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01944)
*Liming Tan,Ye Chen,Hao Zhang,Lirong Qian,Feifei Li,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniMoCa框架，通过Motion-Camera Visual Proxy（MCVP）这一新颖表示，将3D人体运动与相机轨迹统一到视觉空间，取代了传统异构的视觉-参数化控制接口，实现了多人物场景下一致的人体运动与相机联合控制，并在Wan2.2 I2V上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法中，人体运动控制依赖骨架图、姿态图等视觉序列，而相机控制依赖相机嵌入等非视觉参数，这种异构控制接口导致模型难以协调像素对齐的视觉线索与几何嵌入，运动-相机归因困难且对相机估计误差敏感，尤其是在多人场景、大动作、遮挡和动态相机条件下问题更为突出。

Method: UniMoCa的核心是MCVP（Motion-Camera Visual Proxy），一种可共享的新颖表示：从驱动视频中提取3D人体运动与相机轨迹，在恢复的相机轨迹下渲染时间对齐的人体几何，并添加显式相机轨迹标记，将异构的视觉-参数化控制统一为可区分的视觉线索。同时构建了MCVP-Video数据集，覆盖复杂动作、多人交互和多样相机轨迹。

Result: 基于Wan2.2 I2V的实验表明，UniMoCa在人体运动控制、相机控制、时间一致性和相机感知鲁棒性方面均取得了显著提升，且额外复杂度极低。

Conclusion: 通过将运动和相机控制统一到同一视觉空间，UniMoCa使两种控制因子相互兼容而非异构，实现了更一致的联合推理与编辑，为面向人类的视频生成提供了更鲁棒、更简洁的控制方案。

Abstract: Controlling human motion and camera movement is essential for faithful human-oriented video generation, yet remains challenging in multi-person scenes with large body motions, occlusions, and dynamic cameras. Existing pipelines typically rely on visual motion sequences, such as skeleton maps, pose maps, or rendered body representations, for motion control, while using camera embeddings for camera control. Such heterogeneous control interfaces force video generation models to reconcile pixel-aligned visual cues with non-visual geometric embeddings, making motion-camera attribution difficult and sensitive to camera estimation errors. We propose \textbf{UniMoCa}, a representation-driven framework that unifies motion and camera controls in visual space. At the core of UniMoCa is \textbf{Motion-Camera Visual Proxy} (\textbf{MCVP}), a mutually-sharable novel representation that converts 3D human motion and camera trajectories extracted from driving videos into an identity-neutral visual proxy. MCVP renders temporally aligned human geometry under the recovered camera trajectory and augments it with explicit camera trajectory markers, replacing heterogeneous visual-parametric controls with distinguishable visual cues. As both control factors are represented in the same visual space, they become mutually compatible rather than heterogeneous, enabling consistent joint reasoning and editing during video generation. We further curate a \textbf{MCVP-Video} dataset covering complex actions, multi-person interactions, and diverse camera trajectories. Experiments based on the Wan2.2 I2V show that UniMoCa achieves substantial gains in human motion control, camera control, temporal consistency, and camera-aware robustness with minimal additional complexity. More details are shown in our Project page: https://tanliming-daniel.github.io/UniMoCa/.

</details>


### [204] [Event ActivityNet: A Large-Scale Simulated-Event Benchmark for Untrimmed Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01948)
*Cheng-Yao Hong,Ting-Wei Lin,Yun-Chung Lai,Hua-Wei Lee,Hwann-Tzong Chen,Tyng-Luh Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Event ActivityNet，一个基于ActivityNet视频生成的大规模模拟事件流基准，包含3,263个视频、200个动作类别和106.94小时数据，支持长时程事件动作理解、在线时序定位和事件-语言对齐等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程事件流动作理解研究不足，现有数据集多为短视频片段，而采集带密集时间标注的原生事件流数据成本高昂，缺乏适用于长时程事件建模的大规模基准。

Method: 从人类标注的非裁剪ActivityNet视频生成模拟事件流，构建事件体素表示（5-bin和9-bin），保留帧率元数据用于时间映射，使用动作中心重建LPIPS作为内容保留质量的软诊断指标，并提出渐进式嵌套尺度训练协议。

Result: 在渐进式嵌套尺度训练下，识别Top-1准确率从52.25提升至66.42，在线时序定位平均mAP从21.7提升至29.0；分阶段预训练加原生事件微调在多种监督预算下均优于仅目标域和联合从头训练。

Conclusion: Event ActivityNet为长时程事件建模提供了可扩展的基准，但原生相机采集的数据评估对于部署场景的结论仍然必要。
