  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动长波红外（LWIR）测量缺乏精细纹理，现有热纹理成像方法依赖光谱传感或配准辅助模态，存在数据吞吐量大或易受跨模态退化影响的问题。作者希望在不依赖额外光谱或辅助模态的前提下，利用稀疏主动照明实现高质量热纹理成像。

Method: 将热纹理定义为源开观测与其对应源关被动状态之间的残差；在稀疏LWIR照明和快速准稳态配对采集下，该残差可衰减被动发射背景并近似源诱导反射响应。方法分两阶段：阶段1从未观测的被动帧估计每个主动时刻的源关被动状态以获取可靠差分纹理锚点；阶段2结合稀疏锚点与目标时刻附近的被动结构上下文重建密集序列。

Result: 在模拟基准上，T²exture相对AMT-L仅增加0.20M参数，PSNR提升6.66dB；在模拟和真实采集数据上的广泛评估显示，相比代表性VFI基线具有更清晰纹理恢复和更强结构保持能力。

Conclusion: T²exture为稀疏主动采集条件下的热纹理成像提供了实用框架，验证了稀疏主动扰动与被动结构上下文结合的有效性。

Abstract: Thermal imaging remains effective under adverse illumination, yet passive long-wave infrared (LWIR) measurements often lack fine texture. Existing thermal texture imaging approaches commonly rely on spectral sensing or registered auxiliary modalities, incurring substantial data throughput or vulnerability to cross-modal degradation. We introduce T$^2$exture, a sparsely perturbed thermal texture imaging framework that aims to reconstruct temporally dense thermal texture sequences from densely sampled passive frames and a few actively perturbed keyframes. We define thermal texture as the residual between a source-on observation and its corresponding source-off passive state. Under sparse LWIR illumination and rapid quasi-steady paired acquisition, this residual attenuates the passive-emission background and approximates a source-induced reflected response, exposing localized material- and geometry-dependent texture. T$^2$exture reconstructs a dense sequence of this source-conditioned response through two stages. Stage 1 estimates the unobserved source-off passive state at each active instant from neighboring passive frames to obtain reliable differential texture anchors. Stage 2 combines sparse anchors with passive structural context near each target time to reconstruct the dense sequence. On the simulated benchmark, T$^2$exture adds only 0.20M parameters to AMT-L while improving PSNR by 6.66 dB. Extensive evaluations on simulated and real acquisitions further show clearer texture recovery and stronger structural preservation than representative VFI baselines. These results establish T$^2$exture as a practical framework for thermal texture imaging under sparse active acquisition.

</details>


### [239] [RSC-GestureNet: Reliability-Aware Selective Causal Recognition of Chinese Traffic Police Gestures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02200)
*Cheng Li,Renjun Gao,Boyi Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSC-GestureNet，一种可靠性感知的选择性因果识别器，用于中文交通警察手势识别。该模型将姿态置信度作为首要信号，通过因果时序聚合和可靠性感知推理规则，实现早期、稳定且鲁棒的命令识别，在CTPGesture v1数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通警察手势是自动驾驶中安全关键的感知线索。现有识别器需要从连续全帧视频中因果地推断命令，在过渡性肢体运动中保持稳定，并且不能过度信任受损的姿态测量。这些问题促使需要一种显式建模姿态可靠性的识别方法。

Method: RSC-GestureNet的核心方法包括：(1) 将姿态置信度作为首要信号，在图推理过程中对不可靠关节点进行降权；(2) 因果地聚合时序证据；(3) 通过可靠性感知的推理规则选择性发出校准预测。此外还引入了CTPGesture-C特征级损坏基准（七种姿态/RGB退化类型）和RGB级诊断方案。

Result: 在CTPGesture v1完整官方分割（134,424个标注帧和33,451个因果窗口）上，RSC-GestureNet达到93.33%±0.24%准确率、91.71%±0.27%宏F1、91.69%±0.29%在线宏F1、98.80%±0.07% Early@10、0.153±0.013秒TTC，以及最优鲁棒宏F1。相比MD-GCN和HLP-GCN基线分别提升了3.23-4.11宏F1点和2.15-3.07在线F1点。

Conclusion: 通过校准、选择性风险、统计、自适应分支和图像级重提取等多方面分析表明，显式姿态可靠性建模能够改善交通命令识别的早发性、稳定性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Traffic police gestures are safety-critical perception cues for autonomous driving. A deployable recognizer must infer commands causally from continuous full-frame video, remain stable around transitional arm motion, and avoid over-trusting corrupted pose measurements. This study presents RSC-GestureNet, a reliability-aware selective causal recognizer, for Chinese traffic police gestures. The model treats pose confidence as a first-class signal: unreliable joints are down weighted during graph reasoning, temporal evidence is aggregated causally, and calibrated predictions are selectively emitted through a reliability-aware inference rule. We further introduce CTPGesture-C, a reproducible feature-level corruption benchmark with seven pose/RGB degradation families, and an RGB-level diagnostic in which corrupted frames are reprocessed by MediaPipe before recognition. On the complete official CTPGesture v1 split (134,424 labeled frames and 33,451 causal windows), RSC-GestureNet achieves 93.33+-0.24% accuracy, 91.71+-0.27% macro-F1, 91.69+-0.29% online macro-F1, 98.80+-0.07% Early@10, 0.153+-0.013 s TTC, and the best robust macro-F1 among evaluated methods. Under the same split and causal protocol, it exceeds reproduced traffic-specific MD-GCN and HLP-GCN baselines by 3.23-4.11 macro-F1 points and 2.15-3.07 online-F1 points. These results, together with calibration, selective-risk, statistical, adaptive-branching, and image-level re-extraction analyses, indicate that explicit pose-reliability modeling improves early, stable, and robust traffic-command recognition.

</details>


### [240] [CLEAR: Conflict-aware Learning via Evidence-guided Adaptive Routing for Unified Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02206)
*Hantang Li,Qiang Zhu,Xiandong Meng,Debin Zhao,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLEAR是首个用于稀疏视角3D高斯泼溅超分辨率的统一单阶段框架。它通过联合优化真实低分辨率观测与外部高分辨率先验，并引入证据引导的自适应路由机制来缓解梯度冲突、恢复高频细节，在合成和真实4倍超分辨率基准上达到最先进的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏视角超分辨率方法采用两阶段流水线（先低分辨率高斯重建，再高分辨率高斯细化），导致级联式的高斯传递和重建误差累积。因此需要一个统一的单阶段框架来避免这一问题。

Method: 提出CLEAR框架：(1) 在统一高斯表示中联合优化真实LR观测与外部HR先验；(2) 高斯级冲突感知优化策略，以LR梯度为可靠锚点，仅对严重HR冲突进行证据条件软校正；(3) 证据引导的Patch-to-Gaussian路由机制，估计补丁可靠性和细节需求，路由高频梯度和致密化；(4) 共享高斯dropout和分离的中期训练锚定增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界的4倍超分辨率基准上，CLEAR一致地达到了最先进的渲染质量和优越的几何保真度。

Conclusion: 通过统一单阶段框架和证据引导的冲突感知优化，CLEAR有效解决了稀疏视角3D高斯超分辨率中的误差累积和梯度冲突问题，实现了高质量的渲染和几何重建。

Abstract: Sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting Super-resolution is highly challenging since the sparse and low-resolution (LR) inputs lack sufficient geometric and high-frequency information for accurate reconstruction. To achieve high-quality reconstruction, existing sparse-view super-resolution methods adhere to two-stage pipeline that performs LR Gaussian reconstruction and then high-resolution (HR) Gaussian refinement, which directly results in stage-wise Gaussian transfer and reconstruction error accumulation. To this end, we propose CLEAR, a Conflict-aware Learning via Evidence-guided Adaptive Routing, as the first unified single-stage framework for Sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting Super-resolution. Specifically, CLEAR performs joint the optimization of authentic LR observations and external HR priors within a unified Gaussian representation. To mitigate the gradient conflicts introduced by sparse supervision during training, we propose a Gaussian-wise conflict-aware optimization strategy that regards the LR gradient as a reliable anchor and applies evidence-conditioned soft correction only to severe HR conflicts. Moreover, to recover high-frequency details, we introduce an evidence-guided Patch-to-Gaussian routing mechanism which estimates patch reliability and detail demand, lifts them into Gaussian space, and selectively routes high-frequency gradients and densification. Finally, we employ shared Gaussian dropout and a detached mid-training anchoring to enhance the robustness of training framework. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world $4\times$ super-resolution benchmarks demonstrate that CLEAR consistently achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality and superior geometric fidelity.

</details>


### [241] [Self-supervised DXA representations encode multi-system disease risk, biological aging and heritability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02208)
*Gil Sasson,Zachary Levine,Smadar Shilo,Sarah Kohn,Guy Lutsker,Anastasia Godneva,Adam Gabet,David Krongauz,Adina Weinberger,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero,Eran Segal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LeDXA，一个基于JEPA架构的自监督视觉模型，利用全身双能X射线吸收测定法(DXA)图像的原始空间结构来表征系统健康。模型在11,540张无标注扫描图上从头训练，在47,400张外部UK Biobank扫描图上评估，在疾病预测、生物年龄估计和遗传分析等方面均优于传统DXA测量值和通用模型DINOv3，且仅需极少的训练数据和参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DXA扫描通常仅用于测量骨密度和区域身体成分，其丰富的空间结构信息被大量浪费。作者希望通过自监督学习将这些被忽视的图像信息转化为系统健康表征，以提升疾病预测和健康监测能力。

Method: 基于联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)构建视觉模型LeDXA，通过预测潜在表示而非重构像素进行自监督学习。模型从11,540张无标注的Human Phenotype Project扫描图从头训练，并在47,400张外部UK Biobank扫描图上进行跨队列验证。

Result: LeDXA在疾病和生物标志物预测上优于传统DXA测量值和DINOv3，训练图像少约15万倍、参数少约40倍；外部年龄预测r=0.88(MAE=2.90年)；生物年龄差距与疾病负担和45%更高的死亡风险相关；HRT可缩小该差距；嵌入的遗传力高于DINOv3。

Conclusion: DXA图像中包含传统读数未利用的预后信息，且这些信息可通过较少数据和适中计算量学习获得，为DXA扫描的临床价值挖掘提供了新途径。

Abstract: Whole-body dual-energy X-ray absorptiometry (DXA) scans are routinely acquired to measure bone density and regional body composition, leaving their spatial structure largely unused. Here, we show that self-supervised learning (SSL) can convert raw DXA images into representations of systemic health. We introduce LeDXA, a vision model based on a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) that learns by predicting latent representations rather than reconstructing pixels. Trained from scratch on 11,540 unlabeled Human Phenotype Project scans, LeDXA was evaluated internally and on 47,400 external UK Biobank (UKBB) scans. It improved cross-cohort prediction of prevalent diseases and biomarkers beyond scanner-derived DXA measurements and DINOv3, a state-of-the-art general-purpose model, despite approximately 150,000-fold fewer training images and nearly 40-fold fewer parameters. Over a median 4.3-year UKBB follow-up, LeDXA improved incident disease prediction over tabular DXA measures, with the largest gains for hip and knee arthrosis and type 2 diabetes. For hip arthrosis, 66% of incident cases occurred in the highest-risk quartile versus 41% for tabular measures. Its representations predicted chronological age externally (r = 0.88; mean absolute error = 2.90 years), and the biological-age gap tracked broader disease burden and a 45% higher mortality hazard in the oldest-appearing quartile. The gap also decreased in women after starting hormone-replacement therapy, suggesting it may be modifiable. Genome-wide associations recovered mostly known body-composition and bone-density loci, and LeDXA embeddings were more heritable than DINOv3's. These findings reveal prognostic information in DXA images that conventional readouts discard, learnable with relatively little data and modest compute.

</details>


### [242] [VARPose: Flexible 2D Pose Densification via Visual Autoregressive Modeling for Enhanced 3D Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02214)
*Kaiyuan Pu,Tiantian Yang,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VARPose，首次将视觉自回归建模（VAR）应用于人体骨架等拓扑结构数据。通过Granularity-agnostic Pose Tokenizer（GPT）将不同密度的2D姿态编码为统一的多尺度离散表示，并利用UniSkelar自回归模型在粗到细的框架下预测下一密度级别的token序列，实现2D姿态自适密化，为3D姿态估计和人体网格恢复等下游任务带来性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉自回归建模（VAR）在自然图像生成中通过next-scale预测取得了优秀成果，但尚未被应用于人体骨架等具有拓扑结构的数据。现有方法未能有效利用2D稀疏姿态的层次化信息进行3D提升，因此需要探索VAR框架在姿态数据上的适配与扩展。

Method: 提出两大核心组件：(1) Granularity-agnostic Pose Tokenizer (GPT)，采用单一混合码本和残差量化策略，将不同密度的姿态编码为统一的多尺度离散表示，并支持解耦表示与投影，从而可用冻结码本解码新的姿态粒度；(2) UniSkelar，一个统一的自回归模型，将"关节密度"概念化为"尺度"，以最稀疏姿态为条件，在粗到细的方式下逐步预测下一密度级别的token序列。

Result: VARPose在姿态生成任务上超越现有最先进方法，能够泛化到未见过的姿态粒度，并在下游任务（如3D姿态估计、人体网格恢复）中通过2D姿态密化带来显著性能提升。

Conclusion: VARPose成功地将视觉自回归建模范式扩展到人体骨架数据，证明了基于尺度的自回归预测在拓扑结构化数据上的有效性，为姿态建模提供了一种新的统一表示与生成框架。

Abstract: Visual AutoRegressive Modeling (VAR) has excelled in natural image generation via next-scale prediction, but its use on topology-structured data like human skeletons is still unexplored. VARPose is proposed to adaptively densify 2D sparse poses, thereby enriching the anatomical information available for 3D lifting models. Our core contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a Granularity-agnostic Pose Tokenizer (GPT), which employs a single hybrid codebook and a residual quantization strategy to encode poses of varying densities into a unified, multi-scale discrete representation. Our results demonstrate the strong generalizability of this representation. By decoupling the representation from the projection, we can successfully decode novel pose granularities using a frozen codebook with a retrained decoder. Second, we propose UniSkelar, a unified autoregressive model that treats "joint density" as "scale". UniSkelar learns to predict the token sequence for the next density level in a coarse-to-fine manner, conditioned on the sparsest pose. VARPose not only outperforms state-of-the-art methods and generalizes to unseen granularities, but also confers tangible performance gains on downstream tasks, such as 3D Pose Estimation and Human Mesh Recovery, through 2D pose densification. Our code and model are available at https://github.com/BRL-SYSU/VARPose.git.

</details>


### [243] [Local Margin Restoration for Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02216)
*Yan Huang,Guowei Wang,Xu Wang,Kangjun Liu,Xin Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LMR（Local Margin Restoration），一个轻量级单步测试时自适应框架，用于解决视觉语言模型（如CLIP）在测试流中持续适应时面临的错误累积和模式坍缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在测试时分布偏移下性能严重下降；传统的top-1-centric更新会破坏相关类别间的局部语义几何结构，而迭代适应会加剧渐进式偏差累积，最终导致模式坍缩。

Method: 提出LMR框架，包含样本级别的Protected Margin Restoration (PMR)目标，通过保护可能的近顶部候选免受外部难负样本干扰来恢复局部语义几何；同时引入双阶段稳定机制，包括Adaptive Margin (AM)控制器和Bias Correction (BC)，动态打破渐进式偏差累积并防止模式坍缩。

Result: 在CIFAR-C、ImageNet-C和ImageNet变体上的大量实验表明，LMR持续优于最先进的TTA基线，且在低批量测试时场景下表现出卓越的鲁棒性和效率。

Conclusion: LMR通过同时解决样本级别的局部语义几何破坏和流级别的渐进偏差累积，为VLM的测试时自适应提供了一种高效且稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP exhibit remarkable zero-shot capabilities, yet their performance frequently degrades sharply under unexpected test-time distribution shifts. While Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) offers a promising solution, continuously adapting VLMs over an unlabeled test stream presents fundamental challenges. Conventional top-1-centric updates often reinforce errors by corrupting the local semantic geometry among related classes, while iterative adaptation exacerbates progressive bias accumulation, ultimately driving the model toward mode collapse. To overcome these coupled vulnerabilities, we propose Local Margin Restoration (LMR), a lightweight, one-step TTA framework. At the sample level, our Protected Margin Restoration (PMR) objective recovers local semantic geometry by shielding plausible near-top candidates from external hard negatives. Concurrently, to combat stream-level degradation, we introduce a dual-stage stabilization mechanism, featuring an Adaptive Margin (AM) controller and Bias Correction (BC), to dynamically disrupt progressive bias accumulation and prevent mode collapse. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-C, ImageNet-C, and ImageNet variants demonstrate that LMR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art TTA baselines, proving exceptionally robust and efficient even in challenging low-batch test-time regimes. Our code is available at https://github.com/DennisHuangYan/LMR.

</details>


### [244] [VC-Tooler: Learning Compositional and Adaptive Visual Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02217)
*Yizheng Wu,Jiashen Hua,Bing Deng,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VC-Tooler，一种将视觉工具使用作为组合性和适应性能力进行学习的框架。通过分层合成流水线构建轨迹库，覆盖单工具接地、多工具组合和多样化工具上下文三个能力层级，并采用两阶段训练（监督冷启动+强化学习），在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态推理方法过度聚焦于固定工具空间和刚性调用模式下的接地能力，未充分解决工具组合和适应工具返回观测的能力，导致智能体在多步骤推理和动态调整方面存在不足。

Method: 1) 构建层级合成轨迹库，覆盖三种能力层级：单工具接地、多工具组合、多样化工具上下文与接口。2) 两阶段训练：先通过监督学习冷启动建立基础能力，再通过强化学习优化准确、高效且上下文感知的视觉工具使用策略。

Result: 在通用和智能体基准上均达到开源模型最优性能，V*基准上取得95.8%，VTC-Bench上取得35.3%，并在推理时更丰富的工具设置下展现出良好的迁移能力。

Conclusion: VC-Tooler验证了将视觉工具使用建模为组合性与适应性能力的重要性，两阶段训练策略能有效提升多步工具推理性能，且具备向更丰富工具环境的泛化潜力。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal reasoning extends passive image understanding by allowing VLMs to actively acquire and refine visual evidence through visual tool interactions. Effective visual tool use requires three capabilities: grounding tool calls in visual context, composing tools across multiple steps, and adapting reasoning to tool-returned observations. However, existing approaches largely focus on grounding within fixed tool spaces and rigid invocation patterns, leaving composition and adaptation insufficiently addressed. We present VC-Tooler, which learns visual tool use as a compositional and adaptive capability. To this end, we first build a trajectory bank through a hierarchical synthesis pipeline covering three capability levels: single-tool grounding, multi-tool composition, and diverse tool contexts and interfaces. We then train the model in two stages: a supervised cold start that establishes these capabilities, followed by reinforcement learning that encourages accurate, efficient, and context-aware visual tool use. VC-Tooler achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models on both general-purpose and agentic benchmarks, including $95.8\%$ on V* and $35.3\%$ on VTC-Bench, and shows promising transfer under richer tool settings at inference time. Project page: https://w1zheng.github.io/VC-Tooler

</details>


### [245] [GenPrior: Unleashing Text-to-Motion Generative Priors for Zero-Shot Skeleton-based Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02236)
*Jidong Kuang,Hongsong Wang,Jie Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenPrior框架，首次利用预训练Text-to-Motion (T2M)模型的生成先验来弥合零样本骨架动作识别中的语义-运动学鸿沟，在NTU-60、NTU-120和PKU-MMD上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本骨架动作识别方法依赖文本派生原型，但这些原型缺乏几何结构和物理约束，导致语义与运动学特征之间存在明显鸿沟，影响识别性能。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) Dispersion-Gated Feature Fusion，从T2M生成的运动序列中提取运动学原型和类内离散度，通过学习的门控网络自适应地将可靠结构线索注入文本嵌入并抑制合成伪影；(2) Generative Prototype Refinement，利用增强后的原型作为锚点挖掘高置信度的未见类别样本，将类别原型校准到真实分布。

Result: 在NTU-60、NTU-120和PKU-MMD三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，GenPrior在零样本和广义零样本设置下均达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 利用T2M生成模型的先验知识可以有效弥补零样本骨架动作识别中的语义-运动学鸿沟，同时需要精心设计的门控融合和原型精炼机制来确保生成信息的可靠注入与利用。

Abstract: Zero-shot skeleton-based action recognition (ZSAR) aims to recognize unseen action categories by aligning skeleton features with textual semantics. However, existing methods rely on text-derived prototypes that inherently lack geometric structure and physical constraints, resulting in a pronounced \textit{semantic-kinematic gap}. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{GenPrior}, the first framework to exploit generative priors from pre-trained Text-to-Motion (T2M) models for ZSAR. Specifically, we introduce Dispersion-Gated Feature Fusion, which distills kinematic prototypes and intra-class dispersion from generative motion sequences and employs a learned gating network to adaptively inject reliable structural cues into textual embeddings while suppressing synthetic artifacts. Furthermore, we propose Generative Prototype Refinement, which leverages these generation-enhanced prototypes as anchors to mine high-confidence unseen samples, calibrating class prototypes toward the true distribution and thereby unleashing strong performance gains. Extensive experiments on NTU-60, NTU-120, and PKU-MMD demonstrate that GenPrior achieves state-of-the-art performance under both zero-shot and generalized zero-shot settings. Code is available at https://github.com/jidongkuang/GenPrior.

</details>


### [246] [HarMoE: Multi-Source Chest Radiograph Pretraining with Dataset-Disentangled Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02252)
*Haozhe Luo,Ziyu Zhou,Shelley Zixin Shu,Mauricio Reyes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HarMoE框架，通过数据集感知的混合专家模型，将多个多标签分类数据集与MIMIC-CXR报告数据进行统一学习，构建更稳健的胸部X光视觉语言模型，在零样本分类、分布外迁移和定位任务上均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前胸部X光视觉语言模型过度依赖MIMIC-CXR单一数据源进行图像-报告对齐预训练，忽视了多标签分类数据集这一更干净、显式的疾病监督信号来源。然而异构数据集在标签本体、标注协议等方面存在差异，容易导致模型将临床语义与数据集身份混淆，损害迁移性能。

Method: 提出HarMoE（数据集感知混合专家框架）：1）学习跨数据集共享的医学语义，将数据集特有变化限制在深层解码器的轻量级残差专家中；2）采用统一疾病词汇表进行掩码多数据集监督训练，利用互补标注而不引入假阴性；3）构建并发布873k条目的统一数据集。

Result: 在大型胸部X光基准上，HarMoE在零样本分类、分布外迁移和grounding（定位）任务上均一致优于强基线，证明多源结构化知识构建优于单一来源图像-报告对齐。

Conclusion: 构建稳健的放射学视觉语言模型需要超越单一来源的图像-报告对齐范式，转向从异构数据集中进行带有更干净监督和更广覆盖的结构化知识构建。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models for chest X-ray understanding are largely built on image-report alignment and therefore rely heavily on MIMIC-CXR as the dominant pretraining source. While effective at scale, this paradigm underexplores an important alternative source of supervision: a range of existing multi-label classification datasets, which provide cleaner and more explicit disease signals than free-text reports, and can offer broader pathology coverage when combined across sources. However, learning from such heterogeneous datasets is nontrivial, as differences in label ontologies, annotation protocols, acquisition pipelines, and report styles can cause models to entangle clinical semantics with dataset identity, leading to poor transfer despite increased scale. In this work, we revisit radiology VLM construction from the perspective of harmonized multi-source learning. We propose HarMoE, a dataset-aware mixture-of-experts framework that learns shared cross-dataset medical semantics while confining source-specific variation to lightweight residual experts in deeper decoder layers. To further exploit clean supervision from labeled datasets, we train in a unified disease vocabulary with masked multi-dataset supervision, enabling the model to leverage complementary annotations without introducing false negatives. Experiments on large-scale chest X-ray benchmarks show that HarMoE consistently improves zero-shot classification, out-of-distribution transfer, and grounding over strong baselines. Our results suggest that building robust radiology VLMs requires moving beyond single-source image-report alignment toward structured knowledge construction from heterogeneous datasets with cleaner supervision and broader coverage. Code and the 873k harmonized dataset will be released at https://github.com/Roypic/harmoe.

</details>


### [247] [Open-Set Visual Text Forensics via Sparse-Constraint Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02258)
*Jiangling Zhang,Shuxuan Gao,Zeyu Chen,Yichao Liu,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SC-RF（稀疏约束整流流），一种基于生成式模型的视觉文本篡改检测器。它通过估计将查询图像与真实视觉文本统计对齐所需的局部修复成本来定位篡改区域，而非学习伪造特定的决策边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有判别式检测器对特定伪造模式过拟合，难以泛化到未见过的开放式攻击；生成式AI使视觉文本操纵向越来越复杂的方向演变，需要更好的检测方法。

Method: 1) 提出SC-RF（Sparse-Constraint Rectified Flow），一种面向检测器的Flow Matching适应方法，用于空间稀疏异常定位；2) 采用自监督Artifact Injection缓解数据稀缺问题；3) 使用像素空间的Forensic-DiT保留高频取证痕迹。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，F1和IoU分别超过第二名3.2和4.8个百分点；在未见过的文本编辑模式上展现出强零样本性能。

Conclusion: 生成式检测方法能有效泛化到开放集攻击，同时通过局部修复成本估计实现精准篡改定位。此外，该方法还提供辅助压力测试分析视角，可揭示现有检测器依赖的统计线索弱点。

Abstract: Rapidly evolving Generative AI enables sophisticated visual text manipulations that increasingly evade current forensic detectors. Existing discriminative models often overfit specific forgery patterns, limiting their generalization to unseen, open-set attacks. To address this challenge, we propose a generative detector that localizes tampering by estimating the local restoration cost required to align a query image with authentic visual-text statistics, rather than by learning forgery-specific decision boundaries. Specifically, we introduce Sparse-Constraint Rectified Flow (SC-RF), a detector-oriented adaptation of Flow Matching for spatially sparse anomaly localization. We further mitigate data scarcity via self-supervised Artifact Injection and preserve high-frequency forensic traces using a pixel-space Forensic-DiT. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing the runner-up by 3.2 and 4.8 percentage points in F1 and IoU, respectively. In particular, the proposed detector demonstrates strong zero-shot performance on challenging unseen text editing patterns. We further provide an auxiliary stress-test analysis showing that local harmonization produced by our model can weaken the statistical cues relied upon by existing detectors, offering a complementary vulnerability-analysis perspective.

</details>


### [248] [EOVSAM: Efficient Open-Vocabulary Segmentation with SAM 3 in One Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02284)
*Haomin Peng,Yongkang Li,Zhaoxiang Liu,Xiaojie Jin,Shiguo Lian,Yunchao Wei,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EOVSAM框架，将SAM 3从需要穷举类别遍历的缓慢模型改造为单次前向预测的高效开放词汇分割模型，通过去除提示条件化、引入注意力聚合策略，在保持或提升分割精度的同时实现最高338倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 3虽然支持名词短语引导的分割并具有竞争力的开放词汇性能，但其通过穷举词汇遍历实现预测，随着目标类别数量增加计算开销变得难以承受，亟需一种高效的单次预测方案。

Method: EOVSAM通过两步改造实现高效分割：(1) 去除提示条件化，将SAM 3转变为高效的掩码生成器；(2) 引入新的注意力聚合（Attentional Aggregation）策略，端到端优化开放词汇分类，避免多阶段流程和后处理启发式，同时缓解直接优化分类导致的闭集坍缩（closed-set collapse）问题。

Result: EOVSAM在所有评估数据集上相较原始SAM 3一致提升了分割精度，推理速度最高加速338倍；在较低分辨率下仍保持高精度且推理更快。在标准语义分割和全景分割基准上达到竞争性或最先进的精度，同时具备显著的速度优势。

Conclusion: EOVSAM证明了SAM 3可以在不牺牲精度的情况下被改造为高效的单次前向开放词汇分割模型，为开放词汇分割任务提供了兼顾精度与速度的有效方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary segmentation identifies and segments objects from arbitrary textual descriptions. SAM 3 supports noun-phrase-guided segmentation and achieves competitive open-vocabulary performance through exhaustive vocabulary traversal, yet suffers from prohibitive computational overhead as target categories scale. In this paper, we propose an Efficient Open-Vocabulary segmentation framework with SAM 3 (EOVSAM), which adapts SAM 3 for single-pass prediction. EOVSAM removes prompt conditioning to turn SAM 3 into an efficient mask generator and introduces a new Attentional Aggregation strategy to optimize open-vocabulary classification end-to-end. This formulation avoids the multi-stage pipelines and post-processing heuristics commonly used by existing methods, while mitigating the closed-set collapse that can arise when classification is optimized directly. EOVSAM consistently improves segmentation accuracy over vanilla SAM 3 on all evaluated datasets and accelerates inference by up to 338$\times$. Furthermore, EOVSAM maintains high accuracy at lower resolutions while achieving even more remarkable inference speeds. Experiments on standard semantic and panoptic segmentation benchmarks show that EOVSAM combines competitive or state-of-the-art accuracy with a substantial speed advantage over existing open-vocabulary segmentation models. Code and models are available at https://github.com/hustvl/EOVSAM.

</details>


### [249] [Sen-Cap: Sensor-Flexible and Noise-Resilient Human Motion Capture via LiDAR-Camera Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02285)
*Aoru Xue,Yujing Sun,Yiming Ren,Kwok-Yan Lam,Mao Ye,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sen-Cap提出了一种传感器灵活且抗噪声的3D人体动捕框架，融合LiDAR和相机多模态数据，通过统一跨传感器运动估计器和抗噪轨迹追踪器，无需跨传感器标定即可支持任意数量的传感器，并在严重点云噪声下保持鲁棒性，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态传感器的3D人体动捕方法面临两大核心挑战：一是多传感器对齐/匹配依赖显式标定，在视角变化下会产生误差传播，且限制为固定且高度重叠的部署布局；二是现有方法在严重噪声或部分传感器故障下性能退化严重，这在现实环境中很常见。

Method: Sen-Cap包含两个关键组件：1) Unified Across-Sensor Motion Estimator，在人体中心空间重建局部姿态和形状，无需传感器间标定，支持灵活的传感器数量；2) Noise-Resistant Trajectory Tracker，通过迭代细化在严重点云噪声下保持鲁棒性。

Result: Sen-Cap以实时运行的速度，在Human-M3和FreeMotion基准上达到主要指标的最先进性能，并在LiDARHuman26M和RELI11D上展现出强大的跨域性能。

Conclusion: Sen-Cap的传感器灵活性和抗噪鲁棒性使其更适用于现实世界部署，为体育分析、现场机器人和大规模沉浸式环境等场景的动捕应用开辟了新机会。

Abstract: We propose Sen-Cap, a Sensor-Flexible and Noise-Resilient 3D human motion Capture framework that integrates multi-modal data from LiDAR and camera. While multi-modal sensors provide richer information than single-modal sensors, existing approaches still suffer from two core challenges. First, multi-modal alignment/matching across arbitrarily deployed sensors is typically handled by explicit calibration, which propagates errors under changing viewpoints and in turn constrains deployment to fixed, highly overlapped layouts. Second, prior methods degrade under severe noise or partial sensor failures, which are common in real-world environments. To address these challenges, Sen-Cap introduces a Unified Across-Sensor Motion Estimator that reconstructs local pose and shape in a human-centric space without calibrations between sensors, supporting a flexible number of sensors, as well as a Noise-Resistant Trajectory Tracker that maintains robustness under severe point cloud noise through iterative refinement. These sensor-flexible and noise-resilient features make Sen-Cap more practical in real-world deployment. Notably, operating in real time, Sen-Cap achieves state-of-the-art performance on major metrics on Human-M3 and FreeMotion, as well as strong cross-domain performance on LiDARHuman26M and RELI11D. This combination of flexibility and robustness opens new opportunities for motion capture in real-world scenarios, e.g. sports analytics, field robotics, and large-scale immersive environments.

</details>


### [250] [Extended Field of View Analysis for VideoGAN-based Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02289)
*Annajoyce Mariani,Kira Maag,Hanno Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于GAN的语义鸟瞰图交通轨迹生成框架，通过改进语义表示、引入图关联方法替代轨迹提取，并系统研究了更大视野下的生成效果。实验表明该方法可泛化到更复杂交通场景，保持统计上真实且空间关系连贯的轨迹，训练仅需150 GPU小时，推理时间低于20ms，适用于自动驾驶的预测、规划和仿真等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实多样的轨迹生成对实现更高级别的车辆自动化至关重要，而基于规则和经典学习方法难以捕捉交通行为的复杂性，需要探索生成模型在复杂交通场景下的轨迹生成能力。

Method: 基于先前GAN语义鸟瞰图交通生成工作，进行了多项关键扩展：1)改进语义表示；2)用基于图关联的方法替换轨迹提取过程；3)系统研究逐步增大的视野范围；4)引入定量评估框架来评估生成视频中的幻觉和物体持久性问题。

Result: 框架成功泛化到更大更复杂的交通场景，生成轨迹在统计上真实且参与者间空间关系连贯。训练仅需150 GPU小时，对长达20秒的场景推理时间低于20ms，验证了视频GAN在较大交通场景中的高效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于视频的GAN仍然是自动化驾驶中真实轨迹生成的高效且可扩展的方法，适合用于预测、规划和仿真等下游任务。

Abstract: Realistic and diverse trajectory generation is central to enabling higher levels of vehicle automation. While rule-based and classical learning-based methods may struggle to capture the complexity of traffic behavior, generative models have already demonstrated in other fields that they can handle a comparable level of complexity. In this paper, we build upon previous work on generative adversarial network (GAN)-based semantic bird's-eye-view traffic generation and extend the proposed framework in several key aspects. We improve the semantic representation, replace the trajectory extraction procedure with a graph-based association method, and systematically investigate increasingly larger fields of view. In addition, we introduce a quantitative evaluation framework to assess hallucinations and object permanence in generated videos. Our experiments demonstrate that the framework generalizes to larger and more complex traffic scenes while maintaining statistically realistic trajectories and coherent spatial relationships between traffic participants. Within 150GPU hours of training and with inference times below 20ms for scenes of up to 20s, our results demonstrate that video-based GANs remain an efficient and scalable approach for realistic trajectory generation, even in substantially larger traffic scenes, making them well suited for downstream tasks such as prediction, planning, and simulation in automated driving.

</details>


### [251] [SpikeRestormer: Towards Energy-Efficient All-in-One Image Restoration via Unified Event Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02290)
*Shengkai Hu,Jie Shao,Jiaqi Ma,Xu Zhang,Keying Wu,Qilu Zhu,Beihang Song,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikeRestormer，一种基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的全能图像修复（AiOIR）方法，通过内部生成的脉冲事件推理实现低能耗的图像修复，在性能上媲美ANN方法并在SNN方法中达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于ANN的全能图像修复方法虽然统一处理多种退化，但计算成本高，难以实时部署；而SNN作为低功耗替代方案，在静态图像上应用困难，因为缺乏显式事件信号且退化线索与场景结构高度纠缠，难以学习可靠的修复导向脉冲事件。

Method: 提出三个核心模块：(1) 退化事件感知过程，通过减法退化事件注意力（SDEA）提取基于脉冲的退化事件；(2) 层次贝叶斯跳跃掩蔽（HBSM）用于事件可靠性推断；(3) 加性修复事件注意力（AREA）用于修复事件构建。三个互补过程统一形成退化事件感知、可靠性推断和修复事件构建的完整修复流程。

Result: 大量实验表明SpikeRestormer在性能上与ANN方法相当，在SNN方法中取得新的最优结果，同时显著降低能耗。

Conclusion: 通过内部生成脉冲线索进行事件推理，SpikeRestormer释放了SNN在低功耗全能图像修复中的潜力，为SNN在静态图像修复任务中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: ANN-based All-in-One image restoration (AiOIR) unifies diverse degradation handling but incurs high computational costs, limiting its real-time deployment. While Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer a low-power alternative, applying them to static images remains challenging. This difficulty arises because explicit event signals are absent, and degradation cues are heavily entangled with scene structures, hindering the learning of reliable restoration-oriented spike events. To address these issues, we propose SpikeRestormer, an energy-efficient SNN for AiOIR that performs event reasoning over internally generated spike cues. Specifically, we propose a degradation-event perception process to extract spike-based degradation events through Subtractive Degradation Event Attention (SDEA). Moreover, we introduce Hierarchical Bayesian Skip Masking (HBSM) and Additive Restoration Event Attention (AREA) processes for event-reliability inference and restoration-event construction, respectively. By integrating these complementary processes, SpikeRestormer formulates restoration as a unified process of degradation-event perception, degradation-event reliability inference, and restoration-event construction, liberating the potential of SNNs for energy-efficient AiOIR. Extensive experiments show that SpikeRestormer delivers competitive performance against ANN-based methods and establishes new state-of-the-art results among SNN-based methods with significantly lower energy consumption.

</details>


### [252] [A General-Purpose VLM Can Teach an Astronomy Foundation Model to Better Recognize Galaxy Morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02300)
*Dichang Zhang,Jiaqi Deng,Yixuan Shao,Yuanpeng Liu,Jiali Cui,Zhiqiang Lao,Heather Yu,Liang Peng,Simon Birrer,Dimitris Samaras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用通用视觉语言模型(VLM)作为弱监督教师，为天文学基础模型Zoobot提供形态学知识，在有限人工标注预算下显著提升星系形态分类性能，并面向LSST和Roman等未来大规模巡天任务设计了标签高效的适配管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天文学基础模型虽能提供强大的星系表示，但在适应新巡天条件和特定形态识别任务时仍需要大量人工监督，这在高昂的标注成本下构成瓶颈。作者希望探索VLM是否能提供有意义的视觉语义先验作为弱监督信号，以降低对人工标注的依赖。

Method: 首先构建了一个面向巡天的VQA基准，覆盖两个代表性成像域，评估了先进VLM在星系形态问答上的表现。基于评估发现VLM包含有用的形态信号但不完全可靠，作者转而将通用VLM作为形态学教师，对预训练于大规模Galaxy Zoo标注的天文学基础模型Zoobot进行知识蒸馏，在两个巡天域和多种标注预算下进行实验。

Result: VLM在VQA基准上能捕获有用的形态信号和具有信息量的不确定性，但尚不足以替代人工标注者。然而，将VLM作为教师蒸馏到Zoobot后，在两个巡天域和多种标注预算下均一致性地提升了Zoobot的下游形态分类性能，证明VLM知识与天文学基础模型具有互补性。

Conclusion: 通用VLM可以为天文学基础模型提供互补知识，在有限人工监督下教会其更好地识别星系形态。该管线面向LSST和Roman等未来大规模巡天设计，支持标签高效的模型适配，基准和代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing astronomy foundation models provide strong galaxy representations, but adapting them to new survey conditions and survey-specific morphology recognition tasks still requires substantial human supervision. We show that VLM-based VQA systems contain meaningful visual-semantic priors that can serve as weak supervision for downstream morphology classifiers and improve morphology classification under limited human-label budgets. We first introduce a survey-oriented VQA benchmark spanning two representative imaging regimes and evaluate state-of-the-art VLMs on galaxy morphology questions. The results show that these models capture useful morphology signals and informative uncertainty, but are not sufficiently reliable to replace human annotators. Motivated by this finding, we use a general-purpose VLM as a morphology teacher for Zoobot, an astronomy foundation model pretrained on large-scale Galaxy Zoo annotations. Across two survey domains and multiple annotation budgets, the VLM teacher consistently improves Zoobot's downstream morphology classification. These results demonstrate that a general-purpose VLM provides knowledge complementary to an astronomy foundation model and can teach it to better recognize galaxy morphology under limited human supervision. The resulting pipeline is designed for label-efficient adaptation to forthcoming large-scale surveys, including the Vera C. Rubin Observatory's Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST) and the Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope. The benchmark and code are publicly available at https://github.com/fw-ic/VLM-morphology-teacher.

</details>


### [253] [The Push-Forward Transform for Continuous and Robust Comparison of Dynamic Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02306)
*Roua Rouatbi,Juan-Esteban Suarez Cardona,Ivo F. Sbalzarini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Push-Forward变换的形状比较数学框架，通过将形状映射到公共参考域来实现对平移、旋转、反射、重参数化和均匀缩放等变换不变的形状比较，同时保持对内在几何变化的敏感性，并适用于2D、3D及时变形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状比较是图像分析中的基本挑战，现有方法依赖敏感的参数化、地标对应或难以解释和复现的学习表示，缺乏能够对形状不变变换保持鲁棒性、同时保留内在几何信息的统一数学框架。

Method: 将Push-Forward变换应用于符号距离函数（SDF），获得同时捕捉边界和内部几何的连续表示，推导出可解释的形态计量学指标来量化形状相似性，并揭示骨骼拓扑和旋转对称性等特征。

Result: 该方法一致地适用于二维和三维形状，可扩展到时变几何，支持形状与标量场（如强度或分子信号）的联合分析，并在2D、3D及时空数据集上进行了基准测试。

Conclusion: Push-Forward变换为形状比较提供了一种不变、鲁棒且可解释的数学框架，能够捕获形状的内在与外在几何信息，并具有扩展到时间演化和多模态数据的能力。

Abstract: We introduce a mathematical framework for shape comparison based on mapping functions from the shape domain to a common reference domain. This Push-Forward Transform enables invariant and robust comparison of shapes, preserving intrinsic geometric information. Quantitatively comparing shapes and their temporal evolution is a fundamental challenge in image analysis. Meaningful shape comparison requires representations that are invariant to transformations that do not alter shape itself, such as translation, rotation, reflection, re-parametrization, and uniform scaling, while remaining sensitive to intrinsic geometric variation. Existing approaches often rely on sensitive parameterizations, landmark correspondence, or learned representations that are difficult to interpret and reproduce. We show that the Push-Forward Transform (PF-T) applied to Signed Distance Functions (SDFs) yields a continuous representation that captures both boundary and interior geometry. We derive an interpretable morphometric that quantifies shape similarity and reveals features such as skeletal topology and rotational symmetries. The push-forward transform applies consistently to two- and three-dimensional shapes, extends to time-evolving geometries, and supports the joint analysis of shape and additional scalar fields defined over shapes, such as intensity or molecular signals. We present the mathematical formulation, describe an efficient algorithm, and benchmark the approach on 2D, 3D, and temporal data sets.

</details>


### [254] [CalibBEV: LiDAR-Camera Calibration via BEV Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02309)
*Filippo D'Addeo,Lorenzo Cipelli,Adriano Cardace,Emanuele Ghelfi,Andrea Zinelli,Massimo Bertozzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CalibBEV，一种基于鸟瞰图（BEV）的LiDAR-相机标定方法，通过将两种模态统一到共享3D空间表示中，采用两步对齐流程（隐式粗对齐+显式精对齐）实现高精度跨模态标定，在KITTI和nuScenes数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的LiDAR-相机标定方法主要依赖点对像素匹配，精度有限且鲁棒性不足。现有方法缺乏一种能够将两种异构模态数据统一到同一空间表示的框架，因此需要一种更准确、更鲁棒的跨模态标定方法。

Method: CalibBEV首先将LiDAR和相机数据统一到共享的3D空间（BEV表示），使用各自领域特定的网络架构提取传感器专属的BEV特征。标定过程分两步：第一步通过回归直接从BEV特征预测粗标定矩阵（隐式对齐），并利用CLIP启发的对比损失确保两模态BEV特征语义一致性；第二步利用BEV公式显式地将一种模态的特征与另一种对齐，细化粗估计得到最终精确的标定矩阵。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes基准上，CalibBEV相比现有方法将相对旋转误差（RRE）分别降低51%和68%，相对平移误差（RTE）分别降低80%和91%，达到了最先进的标定精度，显著优于先前的点对像素匹配方法。

Conclusion: CalibBEV证明了一种统一BEV空间表示结合两步对齐策略的方法可以有效解决LiDAR-相机跨模态标定问题，相比传统点对像素匹配方法具有显著优势，为跨模态标定提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: We present CalibBEV, a novel Bird's Eye View (BEV) alignment approach for LiDAR-camera calibration. Our method unifies LiDAR and camera data into a shared 3D spatial representation, enabling accurate and robust cross-modal calibration. CalibBEV extracts sensor-wise BEV features from each modality using domain-specific architectures and estimates the calibration matrix through a two-step alignment process. First, we perform an implicit alignment by regressing a coarse calibration matrix directly from the BEV features. To ease this alignment, we enforce semantic consistency between BEV representations across modalities using a contrastive loss inspired by CLIP, guiding both networks toward a unified feature space. In the second step, we leverage our BEV formulation to explicitly align the features of one modality with the other, refining the initial coarse estimate into a final, more accurate calibration matrix. CalibBEV significantly outperforms prior point-to-pixel matching methods, achieving state-of-the-art calibration accuracy. On the KITTI and nuScenes benchmarks, our method reduces the Relative Rotation Error (RRE) by 51% and 68%, and the Relative Translation Error (RTE) by 80% and 91%, respectively, compared to previous methods.

</details>


### [255] [GEOID-Flood: A Large-Scale Multi-Modal Benchmark Dataset for Flood Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02315)
*Gaetano Chiriaco,Luca Barco,Andrea Bragagnolo,Claudio Rossi,Edoardo Arnaudo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了GEOID-Flood，一个大规模多模态洪水分割基准数据集，基于哥白尼应急管理服务数据，涵盖10年间65个国家的219次洪水事件，包含14000多个瓦片的双时相SAR与光学影像及DEM数据。基于该基准评估了基础模型与传统编码器，发现基础模型优势温和但稳定，光学-SAR融合微调效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型的跨区域、跨传感器迁移能力评估依赖于大规模、高质量的多模态基准，但现有洪水测绘数据集很少在规模化层面上结合双时相SAR与配准的光学影像，导致基础模型在该下游任务上的价值未被充分验证。

Method: 构建GEOID-Flood基准数据集：从哥白尼应急管理服务采集219个事件，提供超过14000个瓦片，包含事件前后的Sentinel-1（GRD和RTC格式）、事件前Sentinel-2合成影像、DEM，以及人工验证的标签（区分背景、永久水域和洪泛水域）。在此基础上设计单影像、多时相和多模态三种评测协议，对比基础模型与传统编码器的表现。

Result: 三个主要发现：基础模型相比传统编码器提供了一致但温和的性能优势；光学与SAR融合结合微调在解决瞬时洪水方面效果最佳；在GEOID-Flood上训练的模型对未见事件的迁移能力优于在现有数据集上训练的模型。

Conclusion: GEOID-Flood填补了大规模多模态洪水分割基准的空白，为评估地理空间基础模型在该任务上的价值提供了标准平台，同时验证了多模态融合和领域内训练数据对洪水测绘的重要性。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models aim to learn representations that transfer across regions and sensors, yet evaluating them on specific tasks requires large, high-quality, multi-modal benchmarks that measure how well such models extract value from data. Concerning flood mapping, existing datasets rarely combine bi-temporal SAR and co-registered optical imagery at scale, leaving the value of foundation models for this downstream task largely untested. We introduce GEOID-Flood, a large-scale multi-modal flood segmentation benchmark, derived from Copernicus Emergency Management Service activations, spanning 219 events across 65 countries over ten years. The dataset provides more than 14,000 tiles with co-registered pre- and post-event Sentinel-1, in GRD and RTC format, pre-event Sentinel-2 composite, and DEM, including manually validated labels that separate background from permanent water and flooded water. Using this benchmark, we evaluate foundation models against conventional encoders across single-image, multi-temporal, and multi-modal protocols. We report three main findings: foundation models offer a consistent but modest advantage; optical-SAR fusion with finetuning best resolves transient flooding; and models trained on GEOID-Flood transfer to unseen events better than those trained on existing datasets. Dataset and code available at https://github.com/links-ads/geoid-flood.

</details>


### [256] [Global-Scale Self-Supervised Spatiotemporal Learning for NDVI Time-Series Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02322)
*Ang Li,Menghui Jiang,Xiaobin Guan,Dong Chu,Huanfeng Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GloSSR，一个全球尺度的自监督时空框架，用于NDVI时间序列的重建。该框架通过人工生成含真实云污染模式的降质数据构建自监督训练对，并结合双向Transformer与ConvLSTM的端到端时空学习网络，以及时间-通道注意力重建模块和时空先验约束，在MODIS和AVHRR数据上均取得了优于对比方法的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云污染和噪声导致NDVI时间序列不完整，而深度学习重建方法面临难以获取同一时空位置上的配对清晰与降质NDVI数据的难题，这限制了深度学习方法的应用。

Method: 1) 通过人工模拟真实云污染模式对相对干净的NDVI观测进行降质，构建自监督训练对；2) 设计双向Transformer与ConvLSTM相结合的端到端时空学习网络，同时建模长时序依赖和短期时空相关性；3) 引入时间-通道注意力重建模块增强信息特征；4) 设计时空先验约束以保持细尺度结构和长期物候趋势。

Result: 在MODIS NDVI数据上，人工降质像素重建实验中GloSSR一致优于对比方法；真实观测的时间序列分析显示其能准确刻画植被动态和关键物候状态；长期植被趋势分析和AVHRR数据迁移实验验证了框架的可扩展性和广泛应用性。

Conclusion: GloSSR通过自监督策略有效解决了配对数据难以获取的问题，能够在大规模环境监测中实现准确高效的NDVI时间序列重建，具有广阔的推广前景。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient reconstruction of cloud-contaminated and noise-corrupted NDVI time series remains a challenge in remote sensing. Deep learning provides a promising solution for modeling complex spatiotemporal dependencies; however, its application is often limited by the difficulty of obtaining paired clear-sky and degraded NDVI data for identical spatiotemporal locations. To address this issue, we propose GloSSR, a Global-scale Self-supervised Spatiotemporal framework for NDVI Reconstruction. The framework constructs supervisory signals by artificially degrading relatively clean NDVI observations with realistic cloud contamination patterns, producing self-supervised training pairs that closely mimic real-world degradation. It further introduces an end-to-end spatiotemporal learning network that jointly captures long-range temporal dependencies and short-term spatiotemporal correlation through a bidirectional Transformer with a ConvLSTM architecture. A temporal-channel attention-based reconstruction module is incorporated to enhance informative features, while a spatiotemporal prior constraint is designed to preserve both fine-scale structures and long-term phenological trends during optimization. Extensive evaluations on MODIS NDVI data demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework across both artificial and real-world scenarios. In artificial degraded-pixel reconstruction experiments, GloSSR consistently outperforms the comparison methods. Time-series analyses based on real observations further demonstrate that the proposed framework can accurately characterize vegetation dynamics and capture the key phenological states. Long-term vegetation trend analysis and the transferability analysis to AVHRR data validate the scalability of the framework and illustrate its broad applicability for large-scale environmental monitoring.

</details>


### [257] [Implicit Neural Representations for Multimodal Longitudinal Image Imputation and Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02324)
*Sina Wendrich,Lukas Förner,Zoe Reinke,Kartikay Tehlan,Ansgar Berlis,Michael Frühwald,Matthias Wagner,Thomas Wendler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种患者特定的条件隐式神经表示（INR）框架，用于建模多模态纵向MRI，支持空间和时间插值，并通过随机模态丢弃处理不完整数据，在儿童脑肿瘤患者数据上取得优于线性插值的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实临床中的多模态纵向MRI数据存在序列缺失、采集协议异质、空间分辨率不一致等问题，传统方法需要重采样到固定体素网格，难以充分利用连续时空信息进行精确随访分析。

Method: 提出患者特定的条件隐式神经表示（INR），将多模态纵向MRI建模为世界坐标、时间和模态条件的连续函数；采用随机模态丢弃训练策略处理不完整数据；利用连续的坐标空间公式实现无需固定网格重采样的时空插值；并基于跨模态重建性能推导出自一致性置信度估计器。

Result: 在儿童脑肿瘤患者纵向MRI数据上，T1CE和FLAIR的重建效果显著优于线性插值（p<0.05），T1CE的平均MS-SSIM达到0.95±0.02；预测置信度与真实重建质量高度相关（Pearson r最高达0.996）。

Conclusion: 该框架在异构临床环境中具有可靠的部署潜力，能够有效应对真实世界数据的不完整性并准确量化预测置信度，为肿瘤随访成像提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Longitudinal multiparametric MRI is central to follow-up imaging in oncology, yet real-world clinical data are characterised by missing sequences, heterogeneous acquisition protocols, and varying spatial resolutions across time points. We propose a patient-specific conditional implicit neural representation (INR) that models multimodal longitudinal MRI as a continuous function of world coordinates, time, and modality conditioning. The model is trained with stochastic modality dropout to handle incomplete data, and its continuous coordinate-space formulation enables both spatial and temporal interpolation without resampling to a fixed voxel grid. A self-consistency-based confidence estimator is derived from cross-modal reconstruction performance at inference time. We evaluate the framework on longitudinal MRI from paediatric brain tumour patients, demonstrating statistically significant improvements over linear interpolation for T1CE and FLAIR (p < 0.05), with mean MS-SSIM of 0.95 $\pm$ 0.02 for T1CE. Predicted confidence correlates strongly with true reconstruction quality (Pearson r up to 0.996), suggesting reliable deployment potential in heterogeneous clinical settings.

</details>


### [258] [Context-Aware Mixture of Domain Experts for Bodily Expression of Emotion in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02331)
*Mohammad Mahdi Dehshibi,David Masip*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CA-MoDE（上下文感知混合领域专家）框架用于身体情绪识别，通过专门的场景和物体专家生成领域条件下的情绪分布，作为结构化上下文先验来调制身体专家的预测，并采用最大背书门控策略融合多领域信号，在Body Language Database上取得0.3269的ER分数，超越了仅使用单张静态图像的现有时序模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同的身体姿势在不同上下文语境下可能传达截然不同的情绪，但现有方法仅将场景和物体线索视为辅助特征增强，而非作为情绪合理性的结构化先验，忽略了上下文分布层面的先验信息价值。

Method: CA-MoDE包含三个核心组件：1）专门的场景专家和物体专家，分别生成基于各自领域的情绪类别软分布；2）在分布层面而非特征层面，用这些领域条件软预测作为结构化上下文先验来调制身体专家的预测；3）任务定制的最大背书门控策略，为每个情绪维度选择各专家中最强的上下文信号，避免信号稀释问题。

Result: 在Body Language Database上取得0.3269的Emotion Recognition Score，仅使用单张静态图像即超越了现有的时序模型。

Conclusion: 显式建模结构化空间上下文可以作为视频中行为动态的互补判别代理，证明分布层面的上下文先验建模比特征层面的特征增强更有效。

Abstract: The same body posture can convey entirely different emotions depending on its surrounding context, yet most methods for recognising bodily emotions treat scene and object cues as auxiliary feature augmentations rather than as structured priors over the plausibility of emotions. We introduce the Context-Aware Mixture of Domain Experts (CA-MoDE) for bodily emotion recognition. CA-MoDE incorporates dedicated scene and object experts to generate soft distributions over emotion categories conditioned on their respective domains. These domain-conditioned soft predictions serve as structured contextual priors that modulate the body expert's predictions at the distributional level rather than at the feature level. To fuse these multi-domain signals, we propose a task-tailored max-endorsement gating strategy that selects the strongest contextual signal across experts for each emotion dimension. Our gating strategy mitigates the signal dilution that typically occurs when conflicting or uninformative context distributions are averaged. CA-MoDE achieves an Emotion Recognition Score of 0.3269 on the Body Language Database. By outperforming existing temporal models using only single still images, our framework demonstrates that explicitly modelling structured spatial context can serve as a complementary discriminative proxy for the behavioural dynamics typically captured by video.

</details>


### [259] [Loop-Mamba: A Loop Mamba with Degradation-Aware and Shared Memory for Old Photo Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02346)
*Runci Bai,Yucheng Xin,Pu Wang,Yongcong Wang,Chen Wu,Dianjie Lu,Guijuan Zhang,Pengwen Dai,Guangwei Gao,Siyuan Yao,Zhuoran Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Loop-Mamba，一个轻量级的基于循环状态空间的框架，用于老照片修复。该框架通过持续传播和迭代精化的持久恢复状态来逐步修复老照片中多种耦合退化（划痕、裂纹、褪色、模糊、噪声和缺失区域）。在SynOld基准测试上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老照片常遭受多种耦合退化（划痕、裂纹、褪色、模糊、噪声和缺失区域）的叠加影响，严重损害视觉质量和语义内容。现有迭代式CNN和Transformer修复框架存在计算开销大、梯度稀释等问题，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: Loop-Mamba包含三个核心组件：（1）语义引导退化估计器（SGDE），联合预测局部退化图和全局退化分数，显式建模异构退化；（2）共享结构记忆Mamba（S²M-Mamba），跨迭代维持持久恢复状态，通过共享结构记忆实现持久状态演化；（3）采用一阶状态递归传播潜在恢复状态，而非重复堆叠深层特征变换，缓解梯度稀释并避免迭代CNN/Transformer框架固有的计算开销。此外，采用轻量级多方向扫描策略增强方向信息聚合和结构连续性。同时引入任务导向的老照片损伤恢复评分（ODRS）以联合度量退化恢复和结构重建保真度。

Result: 在公开的SynOld基准测试上，Loop-Mamba在传统修复指标和提出的ODRS评分上均一致优于先前最先进方法。

Conclusion: Loop-Mamba通过循环状态空间范式将老照片修复建模为渐进状态演化过程，以轻量高效的方式实现了优于现有方法的老照片修复性能，为退化模型显式建模和迭代修复提供了新思路。

Abstract: Old photographs often suffer from multiple coupled degradations, including scratches, cracks, fading, blur, noise, and missing regions, severely degrading both visual quality and semantic content. We propose Loop-Mamba, a lightweight loop-based state-space framework that formulates old photo restoration as progressive state evolution, where a persis- tent restoration state is continuously propagated and refined through iterative computation. Specifically, we introduce a Semantic-Guided Degradation Estimator (SGDE) to explicitly model heterogeneous degradations by jointly predicting local degradation maps and global degradation scores, providing degradation-aware guidance for state evolution. We further develop a Shared Structural Memory Mamba (S$^2$M- Mamba), which maintains a persistent restoration state across iterations, enabling persistent state evolution through shared structural memory for robust long-range structural reconstruction. Benefiting from first-order state recursion, Loop-Mamba propagates latent restoration states through recurrent tran- sitions instead of repeatedly stacking deep feature transformations, thereby alleviating gradient dilution while avoiding the computational overhead inherent in iterative CNN- and Transformer-based restoration frameworks. A lightweight multi-directional scanning strategy further enhances direc- tional information aggregation and preserves structural continuity. To better evaluate restoration quality, we introduce the task-oriented Old Photo Damage Recovery Score (ODRS), which jointly measures degradation recovery and structural reconstruction fidelity. Experimental results on the public SynOld benchmark demonstrate that Loop-Mamba consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods across both conventional restoration metrics and the proposed ODRS.

</details>


### [260] [GROVE: Growing and Reasoning over Temporally Stratified Memory from Streaming Video Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02392)
*Sitong Gong,Caixin Kang,Tianyu Yan,Guo Chen,Bo Zheng,Kaipeng Zhang,Yunzhi Zhuge,Xiang Ruan,Huchuan Lu,Yifei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GROVE是一个无需训练的框架，通过因果地从连续视频流中生长单一记忆，同时支持被动问答和主动辅助两种行为。记忆分为多层时间结构（感知证据、时刻、情节、跨日模式），每层配有对应的检索技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴助手既需要回答关于视觉历史的问题，也需要识别历史对当前情境是否有用。现有视频记忆系统主要支持问题条件化的回忆，而主动辅助系统通常使用独立的记忆和控制机制，缺乏统一的框架。

Method: GROVE采用训练自由的框架设计，从连续视频流中因果地构建单一记忆。记忆保留细粒度的感知证据，并逐步整合为带时间戳的时刻、连贯的情节和重复的跨日模式。每一层配有一个尺度原生（scale-native）的检索技能，用于定位观察、回放活动或遍历长期规律。反应式问答和主动辅助共享同一记忆和访问接口，区别仅在于检索是由用户查询还是当前情境触发。

Result: 在包括MM-lifelong和EgoServe在内的多个基准上，GROVE在对比方法中取得了最佳结果。受控消融实验表明时间分层及其访问技能是互补的，其中跨日模式在证据跨越多天时提供了最大的收益。

Conclusion: GROVE证明了通过统一的、分层的因果记忆机制，可以同时支持反应式问答和主动辅助，且不同时间粒度的记忆层具有互补性，跨日模式对长期场景尤为重要。

Abstract: A wearable assistant should both answer questions about its visual history and recognize when that history is useful to the present situation. Existing video-memory systems primarily support question-conditioned recall, whereas proactive assistants typically use separate memory and control mechanisms. We introduce GROVE, a training-free framework that supports both behaviors with one memory grown causally from a continuous video stream. GROVE retains fine-grained perceptual evidence and incrementally consolidates it into time-stamped moments, coherent episodes, and recurring cross-day patterns. Each stratum is paired with a scale-native retrieval skill for locating an observation, replaying an activity, or traversing long-range regularities. Reactive QA and proactive assistance share this memory and access interface, differing in whether retrieval is initiated by a user query or the current situation. Across multiple benchmarks including the challenging MM-lifelong and EgoServe, GROVE achieves the best results among the compared methods. Controlled ablations show that the temporal strata and their access skills are complementary, with patterns providing the largest benefit when evidence spans multiple days. Code will be available at https://github.com/SitongGong/GROVE.

</details>


### [261] [Does Explainability Transfer? A Controlled Benchmark of Attribution Methods on Vision Transformers and CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02396)
*Sathiyamohan Nishankar,Nethmi Pathirana,Pubudu Sanjeewani,Asanka Perera,Selvarajah Thuseethan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个受控基准，系统评估13种XAI归因方法在8种代表性骨干网络（涵盖CNN、各类型ViT等）上的表现，发现归因性能高度依赖架构，基于CNN得出的结论不能可靠迁移到Transformer模型，并揭示了CAM类方法在定位指标上的"饱和假象"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI归因方法有效性的证据大多基于CNN建立，但当前计算机视觉领域已被多样的ViT架构主导，此前结论是否适用于ViT尚无充分验证，需要系统性的跨架构评估。

Method: 构建标准化评估框架，从忠实度、定位、鲁棒性、复杂度和计算成本五个维度，对来自4个算法家族的13种归因方法在8种骨干网络（CNN、各向同性ViT、层次Transformer、混合架构、线性注意力Transformer）上进行受控基准测试，并引入像素级密集掩码评估与常规边界框定位评估进行对比。

Result: 归因性能强烈依赖架构；CNN上的排名无法可靠迁移到Transformer。CAM类方法在CNN和多数ViT上取得最高边界框定位分数，但在线性注意力架构上表现差；密集掩码评估揭示其高分主要源于指标饱和而非准确定位。CAM类方法在全局注意力Transformer上鲁棒性有限，attention rollout一致性稳定但定位差。忠实度相关性在方法间区分度有限。

Conclusion: 现有关于XAI归因性能的主流结论面临挑战，需要架构感知的多维度评估体系来替代单一指标的评价方式。

Abstract: Most evidence on the effectiveness of explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) attribution methods has been established on convolutional neural networks (CNNs), with limited investigation into whether these conclusions generalize to the diverse Vision Transformer (ViT) architectures that now dominate computer vision. This paper presents a controlled benchmark that evaluates attribution quality across five dimensions: faithfulness, localization, robustness, complexity, and computational cost. A standardized framework assesses 13 attribution methods from four algorithmic families on eight representative backbones spanning CNNs, isotropic ViTs, hierarchical transformers, hybrid architectures, and linear-attention transformers. The results show that attribution performance is strongly architecture-dependent and that rankings established on CNNs do not reliably transfer to transformer-based models. CAM-based methods achieve the highest scores under the conventional bounding-box localization metric on CNNs and most ViTs but perform poorly on linear-attention architectures. Pixel-level dense-mask evaluation further reveals that these gains largely reflect metric saturation rather than accurate localization. CAM-based methods also exhibit limited robustness on global-attention transformers, whereas attention rollout provides consistently stable explanations with poor localization. Furthermore, faithfulness correlation offers limited discrimination between attribution methods, highlighting the limitations of single-metric evaluation. These findings challenge prevailing conclusions on attribution performance and demonstrate the need for architecture-aware, multi-dimensional evaluation. The open-source code for the evaluation framework and benchmark results is available at https://github.com/Nishan-Charlie/VIT_XAI_Bench.

</details>


### [262] [USP-Mamba: Unmixing-Derived Spectral and Structural Prompting for Hyperspectral Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02401)
*Shi Chen,Jie Zhang,Yicong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出USP-Mamba框架，通过解混衍生的光谱先验和图像依赖的结构提示来引导Mamba状态演化，解决高光谱图像超分辨率中因果序列建模破坏空间邻接性和状态空间参数化缺乏HSI内在特征对齐的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Mamba-based高光谱超分辨率模型存在两个关键缺陷：(1)因果序列建模需要将二维高光谱特征按预定义扫描顺序展开，破坏了空间邻接性，限制了上下文信息的有效传播；(2)现有模型的状态空间参数化主要来自通用学习表示，未与高光谱图像的固有特征显式对齐。

Method: 提出USP-Mamba框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)解混信息引导的光谱提示，捕获输入图像的全局材料组成并提供持续条件化，注入Mamba序列并逐步跨层适应，引导状态演化朝向组成一致的推理；(2)特征级结构提示，包含空间提示（促进结构敏感的状态编码以保留局部细节）和频率提示（实现均匀区域与高频细节之间的区域自适应转换）；(3)互补的Hilbert扫描和语义引导邻域扫描，保持空间连续性并增强非局部语义依赖建模。

Result: 在不同数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的方法一致地优于代表性方法。

Conclusion: 通过将解混衍生的光谱先验和图像依赖的结构提示融入Mamba状态演化，USP-Mamba有效解决了高光谱超分辨率中空间邻接性破坏和状态空间参数化与HSI特征对齐不足的问题，在多个数据集上取得了领先性能。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image super-resolution aims to reconstruct high-resolution imagery while preserving dense spectral information. Recently, Mamba-based models have shown promising potential for this task by capturing long-range dependencies with linear computational complexity. Nevertheless, their causal sequence modeling requires two-dimensional hyperspectral features to be unfolded along predefined scanning orders, which disrupts spatial adjacency and restricts the effective propagation of contextual information. Moreover, state-space parameterization of existing models is predominantly derived from generic learned representations, without explicit alignment with the intrinsic characteristics of the hyperspectral image. To address this issue, we propose an Unmixing-derived Spectral and Structural Prompting Mamba framework, termed USP-Mamba, which adapts Mamba state evolution through composition-aware spectral priors and image-dependent structural prompts. Specifically, an unmixing-informed spectral prompt captures the global material composition of the input image and provides persistent conditioning throughout reconstruction. Injected into the Mamba sequence and progressively adapted across layers, it steers state evolution toward composition-consistent reconstruction. We introduce feature-level structural prompts comprising spatial and frequency components to provide image-dependent local guidance. The spatial prompt promotes structure-sensitive state encoding for local detail preservation, while the frequency prompt enables region-adaptive transitions between homogeneous regions and high-frequency details. Finally, complementary Hilbert and Semantic-Guided Neighboring scans preserve spatial continuity and strengthen non-local semantic dependency modeling. Extensive experiments on different datasets demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms representative approaches.

</details>


### [263] [Loggia dei Lanzi: AI Thermography Enhancement Comparisons through 3D Photogrammetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02404)
*Scott McAvoy,Jonathan Klingspon,George Bent,Dave Pfaff,Aviral Agarwal,Maurizio Seracini,Falko Kuester*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了2025年12月在佛罗伦萨兰齐凉廊进行的热成像测量活动，使用FLIR T1020 HD相机揭示了隐藏在石膏层下的建筑特征（如被封堵的开口和材料过渡）。研究比较了三种分辨率层级（原生分辨率、FLIR硬件像素移位超分辨率和AI超分辨率模型）在摄影测量SfM流程中对特征检测和三维热模型构建的影响，并提供了公开数据集和交互式3D档案及扩展现实应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 兰齐凉廊是佛罗伦萨最重要的历史建筑之一，其建造历史跨越多个世纪且经历了多次改造。热成像技术能够揭示肉眼不可见的隐藏建筑特征，但如何选择最优的图像分辨率增强方案来生成高精度的三维热模型，是当前文化遗产热成像文档化中的一个关键问题。

Method: 研究于2025年12月使用FLIR T1020 HD热像仪对兰齐凉廊进行热成像采集，利用冬季有利的环境条件获取特征丰富的基准数据。随后在摄影测量SfM流程中比较了三种分辨率层级：原生分辨率、FLIR硬件像素移位超分辨率（UltraMax）以及AI图像超分辨率模型，量化每种方案对特征检测和连接点生成的影响，并评估其对三维热模型密度和精度的影响。

Result: 研究量化了三种分辨率层级在特征检测和连接点生成方面的效果差异，评估了硬件超分辨率和AI超分辨率在实际热摄影测量工作流中是否能产生更密集、更准确的三维热模型。所有数据集已公开，并集成在交互式3D档案框架和城市级扩展现实叠加应用中。

Conclusion: 该研究为人工智能与文化遗产热成像的交叉领域提供了直接贡献，通过硬件微扫描和AI超分辨率在热摄影测量工作流中的系统比较，为文化遗产热成像文档化中的分辨率增强方案选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: The Loggia dei Lanzi in the Piazza della Signoria is one of Florence's most prominent structures visited by millions every year. Its construction history spans multiple centuries of modification. This paper presents the results of a thermal imaging campaign conducted in December 2025, using a FLIR T1020 HD camera, revealing hidden architectural features including walled-up openings and material transitions beneath the plaster surface. The favorable winter ambient conditions provided a feature-rich benchmark upon which to compare the results of enhancement algorithms and artificial intelligence models. We evaluate the application of AI-based image enhancement to thermal heritage documentation through a comparison of three tiers of image resolution in a photogrammetric Structure-from-Motion (SfM) pipeline: native resolution, FLIR's hardware-based pixel-shifted super-resolution (UltraMax), and state of the art AI-upscaled imagery models. We quantify the effect of each resolution tier on feature detection and tie-point generation, assessing whether the additional detail produced by super-resolution, whether hardware or AI-derived, translates into meaningfully denser and more accurate 3D thermal models. Our results contribute to the emerging intersection of artificial intelligence and heritage thermography by providing a direct comparison of hardware microscanning and AI super-resolution within a thermal photogrammetric workflow for cultural heritage. All datasets are made publicly available and accessible within an interactive 3D archival framework, and integrated into a custom citywide extended reality overlay application.

</details>


### [264] [DF$^3$: World Modeling via Decoder-Free Feature Forecasting in Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02428)
*Jiaming Chen,Guoan Xu,Aoshen Huang,Haozhuo Zhang,Yang Li,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DF³是一种无解码器的特征预测框架，完全在潜空间内建模世界演化，通过注入可学习空间查询到冻结的视觉基础模型中直接提取未来状态表征，并采用轻量级运动感知上下文融合机制进行特征对齐与预测，最终通过任务查询直接生成下游任务输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频未来状态预测方法存在计算瓶颈：基于像素的生成方法过度关注与任务无关的细节，计算开销巨大；基于潜空间的方法虽然直接预测特征，但仍依赖重型解码器进行状态到任务的映射，成为计算瓶颈。

Method: DF³框架包括三个关键组件：(1) 将可学习空间查询注入冻结视觉基础模型的末端模块以直接提取未来状态表征；(2) 轻量级、统一的运动感知上下文融合（MACF）机制，结合粗粒度光流变形与细粒度潜空间互相关，使查询与历史token表征交互以对齐和预测下一帧特征；(3) 专门的任务查询对预测特征进行探测以完成下游任务，完全消除解码器需求。

Result: 在公开基准测试和机器人模拟器零样本部署中，DF³达到了与最先进方法相当的性能，同时在集成感知与控制方面展现出更优的效率和灵活性。

Conclusion: DF³验证了完全在潜空间中进行世界建模并直接派生任务输出的可行性，消除了解码器带来的计算瓶颈，为自动驾驶机器人系统的集成感知与控制提供了高效灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Forecasting future states from video sequences is a critical challenge for autonomous robotic systems and a fundamental objective of world modeling. Prior generative methods operating at the pixel level inevitably overemphasize task-irrelevant details, leading to prohibitive computational overhead. While latent-based approaches attempt to mitigate this by predicting features directly, the persistent reliance on heavy decoders for state-to-task mapping remains a computational bottleneck. In this work, we propose Decoder-Free Feature Forecasting (DF$^3$), a novel framework that models world evolution entirely within the latent space and directly derives task outputs, completely eliminating the need for a decoder. Specifically, DF$^3$ injects learnable spatial queries into the terminal blocks of a frozen vision foundation model to extract future state representations directly. By employing a lightweight, unified Motion-Aware Context Fusion (MACF) mechanism that seamlessly integrates coarse flow warping with fine-grained latent cross-correlation, these queries interact with historical token representations to explicitly align and forecast the feature of the next frame. Subsequently, a specialized set of task queries probes these forecasted features for the downstream task. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and zero-shot deployment in a robotic simulator demonstrate that DF$^3$ achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art methods while offering superior efficiency and flexibility for integrated perception and control.

</details>


### [265] [Learning to Tessellate: Point Cloud Generation via Recursive Spectral Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02432)
*Monan Sun,Bangzhen Liu,Huaidong Zhang,Shengfeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointRSP是一个自回归点云生成框架，通过递归谱划分将点云生成重构为保持拓扑的镶嵌过程。它采用拓扑感知的划分自编码器将非结构化点云分解为非平衡二叉树，并通过双流级联生成器和几何校准位置编码实现高质量、高多样性的3D形状生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归点云生成方法依赖启发式标记化策略（如空间排序或随机下采样），这些方法会破坏点云的内在拓扑结构，削弱生成形状的结构连贯性。

Method: 1) 提出拓扑感知的划分自编码器，通过混合递归谱划分策略将非结构化点云分解为非平衡二叉树，提供保持拓扑关系的确定性几何蓝图；2) 提出双流级联生成器，联合建模结构演化与特征合成；3) 设计几何校准位置编码机制，利用多尺度结构中心锚定潜在嵌入，稳定级联生成的早期结构形成阶段。

Result: 大量实验表明PointRSP在生成质量和多样性方面达到最先进性能，并在复杂3D拓扑上展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PointRSP通过拓扑保持的递归谱划分替代启发式标记化，为点云自回归生成提供了一种更结构化的范式，在生成质量、多样性和泛化能力上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Autoregressive models have emerged as an effective paradigm for point cloud generation. However, most existing approaches rely on heuristic tokenization strategies, such as spatial sorting or stochastic downsampling, which often disrupt intrinsic point cloud topology and weaken the structural coherence of the generated shapes. In this paper, we present PointRSP, an autoregressive framework that reformulates point cloud generation as a topology-preserving tessellation process via recursive spectral partitioning. Instead of constructing token sequences heuristically, we introduce a topology-aware partitioning autoencoder that decomposes an unstructured point cloud into a non-balanced binary tree through a hybrid recursive spectral partitioning strategy. This hierarchical representation provides a deterministic geometric blueprint that preserves topological relationships while capturing multiscale structural dependencies within a quantized latent space. To synthesize shapes in this space, we propose a dual-stream cascaded generator that jointly models structural evolution and feature synthesis. In addition, we design a geometry-calibrated positional encoding mechanism that anchors latent embeddings using multi-scale structural centers, which stabilizes cascaded generation during the early stages of structural formation. Extensive experiments show that PointRSP achieves state-of-the-art performance in generation quality and diversity, demonstrating strong generalization across complex 3D topologies.

</details>


### [266] [InfiniSplat: Implicit Gaussian Decoding for Large-Baseline Monocular View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02437)
*Jiawei Wang,Hao Yu,Yongzhen Hu,Xinyi Yang,Tao Ni,Xin Zhan,Junbo Chen,Xiaowei Zhou,Ruizhen Hu,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InfiniSplat是一种前馈式单图像3D高斯泼溅框架，通过从像素对齐表示转向表面对齐表示（几何引导采样+查询条件隐式解码器），在多数据集新视角合成任务上达到最先进性能，并展现出从合成室内数据到开放世界场景的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像前馈3DGS方法受限于像素对齐表示——高斯从固定图像网格位置预测，虽能产生良好的近邻视角渲染，但与场景表面耦合弱，在大视角变化下难以保持一致的结构。

Method: 提出InfiniSplat框架：首先利用几何引导采样根据深度诱导的局部表面结构放置2D支持点；然后通过查询条件隐式解码器从这些支持点的图像特征预测高斯属性。通过将支持点位置锚定在几何结构上，同时将高斯预测与固定像素中心解耦，从而获得更好的表面对齐高斯布局。

Result: 在多个跨数据集新视角合成（NVS）评估中，相比单图像前馈基线方法取得最先进性能；并展示了从Hypersim室内合成训练到复杂开放世界场景的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 表面对齐的高斯表示能够减少网格离散化导致的散射基元，使高斯布局更好地贴合场景表面，从而提升大视角变化下的结构一致性和新视角合成质量，并具备良好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Single-image feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) aims to directly generate a renderable 3D scene representation from one input image, avoiding the cost of multi-view capture and per-scene optimization. However, existing methods are often constrained by a pixel-aligned representation, where Gaussians are predicted from fixed image-grid locations. Such pixel-aligned primitives can produce promising nearby-view renderings, but they remain weakly coupled to underlying scene surfaces and struggle to preserve coherent structures under large viewpoint shifts. We present InfiniSplat, a feed-forward single-image 3DGS framework that moves from a pixel-aligned representation toward a surface-aligned representation. InfiniSplat constructs this representation by first using geometry-guided sampling to place 2D supports according to depth-induced local surface structure, and then applying a query-conditioned implicit decoder to predict Gaussian attributes from the image features queried at these supports.By grounding support locations in geometry while decoupling Gaussian prediction from fixed pixel centers, InfiniSplat produces Gaussian layouts that better follow scene surfaces and reduce scattered primitives caused by grid discretization.Across multiple cross-dataset NVS evaluations, InfiniSplat achieves state-of-the-art performance compared with single-image feed-forward baselines, and demonstrates zero-shot generalization from Hypersim indoor synthetic training to complex open-world scenes.Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/InfiniSplat.

</details>


### [267] [UAV-Based Environmental Monitoring of Rip-Current Indicators Using Wavelet-Derived Texture Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02448)
*Yonatan Ben Avraham,Baruch Binyaminov,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合小波变换与深度学习的物理信息驱动工作流，用于从无人机RGB影像中检测离岸流（裂流）的视觉指示特征。实验表明，集成小波纹理特征相比纯RGB基线模型在分类和检测任务上均有性能提升，双流架构分类准确率超95%，通道替换的YOLOv8检测mAP@50达94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离岸流是威胁海滩游客安全的常见海岸自然灾害，但从标准RGB无人机影像中进行可靠监测十分困难，因为危险通道往往表现为波浪破碎间隙、泡沫纹理或沉积物图案等细微特征，且易受光照、海况和环境噪声干扰。

Method: 提出物理信息驱动的海岸环境监测工作流，将离散小波变换（DWT）衍生的空间频率纹理特征与深度学习集成。评估了多种集成策略：从计算高效的通道替换到带注意力机制的双流融合。使用任务特定的CNN进行图像级存在分类，使用YOLOv8进行目标级定位。

Result: 与纯RGB基线相比，集成小波纹理特征在评估数据条件下显著提升性能。双流架构在分类任务中表现最佳，准确率超95%且召回率高；通道替换方法在YOLOv8目标检测中最有效，mAP@50达到94%。XAI分析证实模型关注与离岸流相关的视觉合理的波浪间隙区域。

Conclusion: 在评估数据集条件下，物理信息驱动的小波集成方法能够支持基于无人机的可解释海滩安全风险缓解决策支持工具。

Abstract: Rip currents are recurrent coastal natural hazards that threaten beachgoers and create operational challenges for lifeguards and coastal managers. Reliable monitoring from standard RGB (red-green-blue) imagery acquired by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) remains difficult because hazardous channels often appear as subtle gaps in breaking waves, foam texture, or sediment patterns, and these signatures are affected by illumination, sea state, and environmental noise. This study presents a physically informed coastal environmental monitoring workflow for detecting visually expressed rip-current indicators that integrates wavelet-derived spatial-frequency texture features with deep learning. We evaluate multiple strategies for incorporating Discrete Wavelet Transform features into convolutional architectures, from computationally efficient channel replacement to dual-stream fusion with attention mechanisms. Performance is assessed against a standard RGB baseline using a task specific convolutional neural network for image-level presence classification and a YOLOv8 model for object-level localization. Under the evaluated dataset conditions, integrating wavelet derived texture features improves performance over RGB-only models. The dual-stream architecture achieves the strongest classification performance, exceeding 95% accuracy with high recall, while channel replacement is most effective for YOLOv8 object detection, reaching 94% mAP@50 for localization. Explainable artificial intelligence analyses provide qualitative evidence that the models attend to visually plausible wave-gap regions associated with rip currents. These results suggest that under the conditions of the evaluated dataset, physically informed wavelet integration may support UAV-based decision-support tools for interpretable beach-safety risk mitigation.

</details>


### [268] [MoRAL: Sensor-Grounded BEV Reasoning for Compact VLMs toward Edge-Oriented Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02449)
*Ambarish Govindarajulu Kaliamurthi,Kaikai Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoRAL，一个两阶段微调流水线，将2B参数的Cosmos-Reason2模型训练为能够基于物理编码的鸟瞰图（BEV）表示进行自动驾驶空间推理。BEV图像将LiDAR距离、物体类别和雷达多普勒速度编码为视觉特征，省去推理时的3D骨干网络。在57,696条链式推理记录上微调后，MoRAL在8类驾驶问题中7类超越零样本8B基线，紧急制动召回率从10.8%提升至47.8%，且可在消费级8GB GPU上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的自动驾驶平台上部署视觉语言模型（VLM）进行安全关键的空间推理，需要同时满足紧凑的模型尺寸和可靠的物理度量锚定。现有方法要么模型过大无法在边缘设备运行，要么缺乏物理编码的空间理解能力。

Method: 两阶段微调流水线：(1) 第一阶段在60,000条接地记录上微调视觉编码器，使其学会解析物理编码的BEV图像（颜色带表示LiDAR距离、簇形态表示物体类别、楔形覆盖层表示雷达多普勒速度）；(2) 第二阶段仅微调52M参数（占总参数量2.4%），在57,696条由8B教师模型生成的链式推理记录上训练，覆盖8种驾驶问题类型。

Result: 在2,304个保留的nuScenes帧上，MoRAL以四倍更少的参数在8类问题中7类超越零样本8B基线；紧急制动召回率从10.8%提升到47.8%；输出退化从94.1%降至20.8%；完整流水线可在8GB消费级GPU上以42 tok/s运行，无需量化。

Conclusion: MoRAL证明将空间感知外化到物理编码的输入图像中，可以消除推理时对3D骨干网络的需求，使小型VLM在边缘平台实现可靠、物理接地、紧凑的自动驾驶空间推理，为移动边缘设备的VLM推理建立了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Deploying vision-language models (VLMs) for safety-critical spatial reasoning on resource-constrained autonomous driving platforms requires both compact model size and reliable metric grounding. We present MoRAL (Multimodal Reasoning for Autonomous Language Models), a two-stage fine-tuning pipeline that teaches Cosmos-Reason2-2B to first read a physics-encoded Bird's Eye View (BEV) representation and then reason over it for driving decisions. The BEV image encodes LiDAR metric distance as color bands, object class as cluster morphology, and radar Doppler velocity as directional wedge overlays, externalizing spatial perception into the input image so that no learned 3D backbone is required at inference. Stage 1 fine-tunes the vision encoder on 60,000 grounding records; zero-shot baselines produce no parseable BEV outputs, confirming the vocabulary requires explicit training. Stage 2 fine-tunes the full model (52M parameters, 2.4% of total) on 57,696 chain-of-thought records generated by Cosmos-Reason2-8B as teacher, spanning eight driving question types. On 2,304 held-out nuScenes frames evaluated by Gemma 4 (31B) calibrated against human review, MoRAL wins seven of eight question types over a zero-shot 8B baseline despite using four times fewer parameters, with the largest margins on question types requiring structured multi-step physics reasoning. Emergency braking recall improves from 10.8% to 47.8%, output degeneration falls from 94.1% to 20.8%, and the full pipeline fits a consumer 8 GB GPU at 42 tok/s without quantization. These results establish a reproducible foundation for compact, physics-grounded VLM reasoning on mobile edge platforms.

</details>


### [269] [ISRS-DETR: Detection-Guided Click Propagation for Remote Sensing Interactive Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02468)
*Thanh Duc Pham,Anh Nguyen,Duong Duc Hieu,Minh-Tan Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ISRS-DETR，一种检测引导的交互式遥感图像分割框架，通过RF-DETR解码器定位同类共现目标并采用动态Top-K点击选择策略，让单次用户交互的信息传播到整个类别，大幅减少每幅图像所需的点击次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遥感图像交互式分割方法将所有同类实例视为单一目标，导致对一个物体的交互无法帮助其同类邻居，一幅图像可能需要多达40次点击，严重限制了框架的实用性。作者观察到遥感场景中物体间存在强烈的相互关联性，单个点击的物体能提供关于其类别其他物体的丰富信息，由此希望利用这种关联性减少交互成本。

Method: ISRS-DETR框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 使用RF-DETR解码器结合交互式分割骨干网络，用于定位同类共现物体；(2) 提出动态Top-K点击选择策略，仅保留可靠的目标提案并将其转化为模拟点击，使一次用户交互能够传播到整个类别。

Result: 在三个标准遥感基准数据集上的实验表明，ISRS-DETR达到了最先进的精度，同时显著减少了每幅图像的点击次数（NoC-I）。

Conclusion: 通过将目标级证据注入训练和推理过程，ISRS-DETR成功利用遥感场景中物体间的强相关性，在保持高分割精度的同时大幅降低交互成本，提升了交互式遥感分割框架的实用性和效率。

Abstract: Interactive segmentation reduces the prohibitive cost of pixel-level annotation by allowing users to delineate objects with a few clicks. However, applying this paradigm directly to remote sensing imagery is non-trivial: ultra-high resolutions, small object sizes, and sparse spatial distributions all degrade segmentation quality. Recent work has addressed the resolution barrier and achieved competitive results in interactive segmentation for remote sensing (ISRS). However, they treat all instances of a class within an image as a single objective target. Consequently, interactions spent on one object contribute nothing to its same-class neighbours, and satisfactory masks may demand up to 40 clicks per image, hindering the practicality of these frameworks. We observe that remote sensing scenes exhibit markedly strong inter-object correlation, meaning a single clicked object is highly informative about the rest of its category. Building on this, we propose ISRS-DETR, a detection-guided interactive segmentation framework that injects object-level evidence into both training and inference. Our ISRS-DETR employs an RF-DETR decoder with the interactive segmentation backbone to localise co-occurring same-class objects, and introduces a Dynamic Top-K Click Selection strategy that retains only reliable proposals and converts each into a simulated click, so one user interaction propagates across an entire class. Experiments on three standard remote sensing benchmarks show that ISRS-DETR achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while substantially reducing Number of Clicks per Image (NoC-I). All codes and data splits will be released for reproducibility upon acceptance.

</details>


### [270] [Calibrated Similarity and Graph Clustering for Open-Set Animal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02469)
*Mohamed ElBassat,Seifeldin Elkerdany,Mohamed ElBialy,Gamal Abouelhamd,Jana Ghoneim,Assem Elkady,Mohamed Elboraay,Nelly Semenova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于AnimalCLEF26动物再识别任务的"相似度到聚类"流水线，通过分割、物种特定预处理、全局-局部特征融合（WildFusion + MiewID + ALIKED/LightGlue + DISK/LightGlue）以及图聚类方法，在四种动物数据集上取得了最佳公开ARI 0.72124和最佳私有ARI 0.71087的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物再识别面临开放集挑战，系统不仅需要将查询图像匹配到已知个体，还需要通过聚类发现未见过的个体。现有方法在野外条件下的视觉变化和挑战性场景中表现有限，需要一种结合物种感知预处理和全局-局部特征融合的有效方案。

Method: 首先通过分割提取目标样本，对猞猁、海龟和火蜥蜴应用轻量级物种特定预处理增强身份相关视觉线索，德州角蜥仅使用分割结果。然后使用WildFusion框架，通过校准和融合MiewID全局描述符与两条局部匹配分支（ALIKED+LightGlue和DISK+LightGlue）估计成对相似度。查询-查询相似度通过图聚类转换为身份簇，查询-数据库相似度用于将置信样本关联到已知身份。评估了无训练和微调的MiewID变体（包括Dynamic ArcFace和SphereFace2-Focal），最终集成组合。

Result: 选定的集成方案显著优于WildFusion基线，获得最佳公开ARI 0.72124和私有ARI 0.70393；一个更简单的预处理后再校准变体获得了最佳私有ARI 0.71087。

Conclusion: 校准的全局-局部特征融合结合物种感知预处理对于开放集野生动物再识别是有效的，代码已开源在GitHub上。

Abstract: AnimalCLEF26 addresses discovery-oriented animal re-identification, where systems must both attach query images to known individuals and discover unseen individuals by clustering them correctly. We present a similarity-to-clustering pipeline for this setting across Eurasian lynx, fire salamander, loggerhead sea turtle, and Texas horned lizard images. The method first isolates the target specimen using segmentation and then applies lightweight species-specific preprocessing for lynx, sea turtle, and salamander images to enhance identity-relevant visual cues, while Texas horned lizard images are used after segmentation only. Pairwise similarities are then estimated with WildFusion by calibrating and combining a MiewID global descriptor with two local matching branches, ALIKED + LightGlue and DISK + LightGlue.
